### [57] [Versioned Late Materialization for Ultra-Long Sequence Training in Recommendation Systems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24806)
*Liang Guo,Ge Song,Litao Deng,Jianhui Sun,Chufeng Hu,Lu Zhang,Zhen Ma,Shouwei Chen,Weiran Liu,Sarang Masti Sreeshylan,Xiaoxuan Meng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"版本化延迟物化"（versioned late materialization）范式，通过将用户交互历史（UIH）存储在规范化的不可变存储层中，并在训练时即时重建序列，从而解决传统"胖行"预物化策略带来的存储和I/O冗余问题。该方案支持O2O一致性保证、多维投影下推、流水线I/O预取等优化，已在生产环境的DLRM、HSTU和ULTRA-HSTU等模型上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代DLRM遵循序列长度的扩展定律，业界标准的"Fat Row"范式将用户交互历史预物化到每个训练样本中，导致数据基础设施使用量超过GPU训练能力。在多租户环境中，不同序列长度需求的模型共享联合数据集时，数据冗余问题被进一步放大，形成存储和I/O瓶颈。

Method: 1. 版本化延迟物化范式：通过轻量级版本化指针在训练时即时重建序列，消除数据冗余
2. 双协议（bifurcated protocol）：确保流式训练和批处理训练的在线-离线（O2O）一致性，防止未来信息泄露
3. 读优化不可变存储层：支持多维投影下推，为异构模型租户提供高效数据访问
4. 分离式数据预处理：流水线I/O预取和数据亲和性优化，掩蔽序列重建延迟

Result: 1. 显著降低训练数据基础设施资源消耗
2. 支持更激进的序列长度扩展
3. 带来显著模型质量提升
4. 保持训练吞吐量为GPU计算瓶颈（而非I/O瓶颈）

Conclusion: 该系统作为现代推荐模型架构（包括HSTU和ULTRA-HSTU）的基础数据基础设施，成功解决了DLRM扩展中面临的存储和I/O瓶颈问题，为长序列推荐模型的训练提供了高效、可持续的数据基础设施支持。

Abstract: Modern Deep Learning Recommendation Models (DLRMs) follow scaling laws with sequence length, driving the frontier toward ultra-long User Interaction History (UIH). However, the industry-standard "Fat Row" paradigm, which pre-materializes these sequences into every training example, creates a storage and I/O wall where data infrastructure usage exceeds GPU training capacity due to data redundancy that is amplified in multi-tenant environments where models with vastly different sequence length requirements share a union dataset. We present a \emph{versioned late materialization} paradigm that eliminates this redundancy by storing UIH once in a normalized, immutable tier and reconstructing sequences just-in-time during training via lightweight versioned pointers. The system ensures Online-to-Offline (O2O) consistency through a bifurcated protocol that prevents future leakage across both streaming and batch training, while a read-optimized immutable storage layer provides multi-dimensional projection pushdown for heterogeneous model tenants. Disaggregated data preprocessing with pipelined I/O prefetching and data-affinity optimizations masks the latency of training-time sequence reconstruction, keeping training throughput compute-bound by GPUs. Deployed on production DLRMs, the system reduces training data infrastructure resource usage while enabling aggressive sequence length scaling that delivers significant model quality gains, serving as the foundational data infrastructure for modern recommendation model architectures, including HSTU and ULTRA-HSTU.

</details>


### [58] [Offline Evaluation Measures of Fairness in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25032)
*Theresia Veronika Rampisela*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了推荐系统公平性评估指标的理论、实证和概念局限性，揭示了现有指标的缺陷（如可解释性差、表达力不足、计算问题），并提出了改进方法和实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着公平AI相关立法的出现，推荐系统公平性评估变得日益重要。然而，现有公平性评估指标缺乏对其鲁棒性的深入分析，导致对指标局限性理解不足，包括：不清楚何种模型输出产生(不)公平分数、分数的经验分布未知、以及某些情况下无法计算（如除零问题）。

Method: 研究涵盖多种离线评估指标，按评估对象（用户/物品）和评估粒度（群体/个体）分类，通过理论分析和实证研究评估指标表现。

Result: 揭示了现有指标在可解释性、表达力和适用性方面的缺陷；提出了克服这些局限性的新评估方法和指标；提供了指标选择的实用指南。

Conclusion: 本论文推动了推荐系统公平性离线评估的技术水平，为实践中的公平性评估指标选择提供了更精确的指导。

Abstract: The evaluation of recommender system fairness has become increasingly important, especially with recent legislation that emphasises the development of fair and responsible artificial intelligence. This has led to the emergence of various fairness evaluation measures, which quantify fairness based on different definitions. However, many of such measures are simply proposed and used without further analysis on their robustness. As a result, there is insufficient understanding and awareness of the measures' limitations. Among other issues, it is not known what kind of model outputs produce the (un)fairest score, how the measure scores are empirically distributed, and whether there are cases where the measures cannot be computed (e.g., due to division by zero). These issues cause difficulty in interpreting the measure scores and confusion on which measure(s) should be used for a specific case.
  This thesis presents a series of papers that assess and overcome various theoretical, empirical, and conceptual limitations of existing recommender system fairness evaluation measures. We investigate a wide range of offline evaluation measures for different fairness notions, divided based on the evaluation subjects (users and items) and for different evaluation granularities (groups of subjects and individual subjects). Firstly, we perform theoretical and empirical analysis on the measures, exposing flaws that limit their interpretability, expressiveness, or applicability. Secondly, we contribute novel evaluation approaches and measures that overcome these limitations. Finally, considering the measures' limitations, we recommend guidelines for the appropriate measure usage, thereby allowing for more precise selection of fairness evaluation measures in practical scenarios.
  Overall, this thesis contributes to advancing the state-of-the-art offline evaluation of fairness in recommender systems.

</details>


### [59] [UnIte: Uncertainty-based Iterative Document Sampling for Domain Adaptation in Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25142)
*Jongyoon Kim,Minseong Hwang,Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UnIte方法，通过区分aleatoric和epistemic两种不确定性来指导文档采样，显著提升无监督域适应中神经检索器的效率和性能，仅需约4k训练样本即可获得显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档采样方法仅关注多样性，忽略模型不确定性，导致pseudo query生成效率低下。核心假设是：模型已熟悉的文档（低epistemic uncertainty）贡献有限，而固有噪声文档（高aleatoric uncertainty）会引入错误信号。

Method: UnIte方法包含两个核心策略：(1)过滤高aleatoric uncertainty（数据固有噪声）的文档；(2)优先选择高epistemic uncertainty（模型知识盲区）的文档。迭代过程中最大化模型当前的学习效用。

Result: 在BEIR大规模语料库上，使用小规模训练样本（平均4k），UnIte分别实现+2.45和+3.49的nDCG@10提升，适用于小模型和大模型。

Conclusion: UnIte通过区分两种不确定性（aleatoric vs epistemic）实现更高效的无监督域适应，显著降低标注需求的同时提升检索性能。

Abstract: Unsupervised domain adaptation generalizes neural retrievers to an unseen domain by generating pseudo queries on target domain documents. The quality and efficiency of this adaptation critically depend on which documents are selected for pseudo query generation. The existing document sampling method focuses on diversity but fails to capture model uncertainty. In contrast, we propose **Un**certainty-based **Ite**rative Document Sampling (UnIte) addressing these limitations by (1) filtering documents with high aleatoric uncertainty and (2) prioritizing those with high epistemic uncertainty, maximizing the learning utility of the current model. We conducted extensive experiments on a large corpus of BEIR with small and large models, showing significant gains of +2.45 and +3.49 nDCG@10 with a smaller training sample size, 4k on average.

</details>


### [60] [From Local Indices to Global Identifiers: Generative Reranking for Recommender Systems via Global Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25291)
*Pengyue Jia,Xiaobei Wang,Yingyi Zhang,Shuchang Liu,Yupeng Hou,Hailan Yang,Xu Gao,Xiaopeng Li,Yejing Wang,Julian McAuley,Xiang Li,Lantao Hu,Yongqi Liu,Kaiqiao Zhan,Han Li,Kun Gai,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GloRank是一种生成式重排序框架，通过将项目表示为离散token序列，将重排序任务从选择局部索引改为生成全局标识符，解决现有方法中输出神经元语义不一致的问题。该方法采用监督预训练+强化学习后训练的优化策略，在公开数据集和工业数据集上均超越现有基线，并在冷启动场景中展现更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排序方法将任务建模为从局部输入列表中选择索引，这种方式存在语义不一致的核心问题：同一输出神经元（logits）在不同样本中代表不同项目，阻碍模型建立稳定、固有的项目理解。该问题源于输入顺序的变异性，导致评分机制无法与一致的标准对齐。

Method: GloRank提出生成式框架，核心创新包括：（1）将项目表示为离散token序列，重排序任务转化为token生成任务；（2）通过全局标识符机制使项目评估基于一致的标准，有效解耦评分机制与可变输入顺序；（3）两阶段优化流程：监督预训练阶段利用高质量演示初始化模型，强化学习后训练阶段直接优化列表级效用函数。

Result: 在两个公开基准数据集和大规模工业数据集上的实验表明，GloRank一致性地超越现有最优基线方法。冷启动场景下展现出更优的鲁棒性。线上A/B测试进一步验证了其实际效果。

Conclusion: GloRank通过引入全局动作空间范式，为重排序任务提供了一种语义一致且可扩展的解决方案。两阶段优化策略有效结合了监督学习的稳定性与强化学习的列表级优化能力。该框架在工业应用场景中具有重要的实用价值和推广意义。

Abstract: In modern recommender systems, list-wise reranking serves as a critical phase within the multi-stage pipeline, finalizing the exposed item sequence and directly impacting user satisfaction by modeling complex intra-list item dependencies. Existing methods typically formulate this task as selecting indices from the local input list. However, this approach suffers from a semantically inconsistent action space: the same output neuron (logits) represents different items across different samples, preventing the model from establishing a stable, intrinsic understanding of the items. To address this, we propose GloRank (Global Action Space Ranker), a generative framework that shifts reranking from selecting local indices to generating global identifiers. Specifically, we represent items as sequences of discrete tokens and reformulate reranking as a token generation task. This design effectively decouples the scoring mechanism from the variable input order, ensuring that items are evaluated against a consistent global standard. We further enhance this with a two-stage optimization pipeline: a supervised pre-training phase to initialize the model with high-quality demonstrations, followed by a reinforcement learning-based post-training phase to directly maximize list-wise utility. Extensive experiments on two public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset, coupled with online A/B tests, demonstrate that GloRank consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and achieves superior robustness in cold-start scenarios.

</details>


### [61] [Stop Using the Wilcoxon Test: Myth, Misconception and Misuse in IR Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25349)
*Julián Urbano*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了IR基准测试中Wilcoxon符号秩检验优于t检验的传统观点，指出统计学教科书在检验假设的表述上存在不一致和遗漏，导致Wilcoxon检验在IR研究中被持续误用。研究通过系统性文献综述和分析表明，Wilcoxon检验在IR场景下无法有效控制第一类误差率，继续使用该检验是不合理的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在IR系统的基准测试中，Wilcoxon符号秩检验通常被视为比t检验更安全的替代方案。这种信念源于教科书和建议将Wilcoxon定位为适当的非参数替代方法，因为指标得分不是正态分布的。研究认为这种说法具有误导性且有害。

Method: 通过系统性文献综述、统计假设分析以及对TREC数据的实证演示，研究揭示了Wilcoxon检验在IR场景下如何以及为何容易失去对第一类误差的控制。

Result: Wilcoxon检验在IR环境中容易失去对第一类误差的控制，几乎必然会导致检验失效并误导研究人员。

Conclusion: 在IR评估中继续使用Wilcoxon检验是不合理的，放弃Wilcoxon检验将提高该领域的方法论严谨性。

Abstract: In benchmarking of Information Retrieval systems, the Wilcoxon signed-rank test is often treated as a safer alternative to the t-test. This belief is fueled by textbooks and recommendations that portray Wilcoxon as the proper non-parametric alternative because metric scores are not normally distributed. We argue that this narrative is misleading and harmful. A careful review of Statistics textbooks reveals inconsistencies and omissions in how the assumptions underlying these tests are presented, fostering confusion that has propagated into IR research. As a result, Wilcoxon has been routinely misapplied for decades, creating a false sense of safety against a threat that was never there to begin with, while introducing another one so severe that it virtually guarantees the test will break down and mislead researchers. Through a combination of systematic literature review, analysis and empirical demonstrations with TREC data, we show how and why the Wilcoxon test easily loses control of its Type I error rate in IR settings. We conclude that the continued use of Wilcoxon in IR evaluation is unjustified and that abandoning it would improve the methodological soundness of our field.

</details>


### [62] [Health System Scale Semantic Search Across Unstructured Clinical Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25605)
*Faith Wavinya Mutinda,Spandana Makeneni,Anna Lin,Shivaji Dutta,Irit R. Rasooly,Patrick Dibussolo,Shivani Kamath Belman,Hessam Shahriari,Kevin Murphy,Alex B. Ruan,Barbara H. Chaiyachati,Sanjay Chainani,Robert W. Grundmeier,Scott M. Haag,Jeffrey M. Miller,Heather M. Griffis,Ian M. Campbell*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在大规模儿童医院成功部署了涵盖1.66亿份临床文档的健康系统级语义搜索系统，采用Qwen3-embedding-0.6B模型，实现了亚秒级查询延迟（单用户237ms，20并发451ms），月度成本约4000美元，临床问答准确率达94.6%，显著提升图表审查效率（缩短24-89%时间）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床信息检索中语义搜索优于关键词匹配，但将其实施于包含数亿份临床文档的整个健康系统面临巨大的工程、成本和治理挑战，导致此前无法推广应用。

Method: 1. 索引1.66亿份临床文档（4.84亿向量），来自168万患者；2. 采用指令微调的qwen3-embedding-0.6B嵌入模型；3. 使用存储优化的托管数据库存储向量；4. 低延迟键值存储维护全文元数据；5. 符合HIPAA的治理框架；6. 通过三个维度评估：嵌入模型和分块策略优化、规模化性能表征（成本、延迟、检索质量）、临床实用性评估（三项任务图表抽象效率对比）。

Result: 1. 查询延迟：单用户中位数237ms，20用户并发451ms；2. 月度成本约4000美元；3. Qwen3嵌入+300-token分块在临床问答基准上达到94.6%准确率；4. 三项临床抽象任务中，语义搜索相比人工图表审查时间缩短24-89%，同时保持相当的人际信度。

Conclusion: 健康系统级语义搜索在技术和运营上均可行。该系统为交互式搜索、人群生成和下游LLM驱动的临床应用提供基础设施支持，且无需专业信息学专业知识。

Abstract: Introduction: Semantic search, which retrieves documents based on conceptual similarity rather than keyword matching, offers substantial advantages for retrieval of clinical information. However, deploying semantic search across entire health systems, comprising hundreds of millions of clinical notes, presents formidable engineering, cost, and governance challenges that have prevented adoption. Methods: We deployed a semantic search system at a large children's hospital indexing 166 million clinical notes (484 million vectors) from 1.68 million patients. The system uses instruction-tuned qwen3-embedding-0.6B embeddings, stores vectors in a managed database with storage-optimized indexing, maintains full-text metadata in a low-latency key-value store, and operates within a HIPAA-compliant governance framework. We evaluated the system through three experiments: optimization of embedding model and chunking strategy using a physician-authored benchmark dataset, characterization of full-scale performance (cost, latency, retrieval quality), and clinical utility assessment via comparison of chart abstraction efficiency across three tasks. Results: The system delivers sub-second query latency (median 237 ms single-user, 451 ms 20-user concurrency) with monthly costs of approximately USD 4,000. Qwen3 embeddings with 300-token chunk size achieved 94.6% accuracy on a clinical question-answering benchmark. In clinical utility evaluation across three abstraction tasks, semantic search reduced time-to-completion by 24 to 89% compared to clinician-performed chart review while maintaining comparable inter-rater agreement. Conclusion: Health-system-scale semantic search is both technically and operationally feasible. The system provides infrastructure supporting interactive search, cohort generation, and downstream LLM-powered clinical applications without requiring specialized informatics expertise.

</details>


### [63] [The Attention Market: Interpreting Online Fair Re-ranking as Manifold Optimization under Walrasian Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25577)
*Chen Xu,Wei Chu,Wenyu Hu,Fengran Mo,Jun Xu,Maarten de Rijke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ManifoldRank算法，通过将公平重排序问题重构为注意力市场框架，利用流形优化理论实现瓦尔拉斯均衡，在供给侧和需求侧分别进行梯度调整，有效解决了现有方法在不同设置下性能不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线公平感知重排序方法在20种设置下表现出显著的性能差异，缺乏对导致这些不一致性的根本原因的理论理解。

Method: 将公平重排序问题形式化为由瓦尔拉斯均衡(Walrasian Equilibrium)支配的注意力市场框架，公平性被视为税收成本。通过流形优化理论，证明寻求均衡等价于在由市场构建的特定排序流形上进行梯度下降。针对不同重排序设置诱导不同流形几何结构的问题，ManifoldRank算法在供给侧基于公平性需求进行梯度调整，在需求侧基于排序分数预测额外的梯度调整项。

Result: 实验结果表明，ManifoldRank在多个数据集上有效平衡了公平性和准确性，验证了该市场驱动的流形优化方法的有效性。

Conclusion: ManifoldRank通过在供给侧和需求侧分别引入梯度调整项，有效平衡了公平性和准确性，在多个数据集上验证了其有效性。该研究为公平排序提供了一个统一的几何优化框架。

Abstract: Fair re-ranking aims to promote long-tail items and enhance diversity within groups in information retrieval. While previous research on online fairness-aware re-ranking has shown promising outcomes, our comprehensive evaluation of online fair re-ranking methods over 20 settings reveals significant performance disparities among existing methods. To uncover the root causes of these inconsistencies, we reformulate fair re-ranking within an attentional market framework governed by a Walrasian Equilibrium, where the fairness is treated as a taxation cost. This market-based formulation is then coupled with manifold optimization, demonstrating that seeking this equilibrium is equivalent to performing gradient descent on a specific ranking manifold constructed by the market. Different re-ranking settings induce distinct manifold geometries, and these intrinsic geometric differences dictate the gradient landscapes and optimization trajectories. We propose ManifoldRank, an efficient online fair re-ranking algorithm. ManifoldRank adjusts gradients to align with the ranking manifold, considering various contextual settings. On the supply side, it incorporates a gradient adjustment based on different fairness requirements, accounting for associated costs. On the demand side, it empirically predicts an additional gradient adjustment term derived from the ranking scores. By integrating these two gradient adjustments, ManifoldRank effectively balances fairness and accuracy. Experimental results across multiple datasets confirm ManifoldRank's effectiveness.

</details>


### [64] [K-CARE: Knowledge-driven Symmetrical Contextual Anchoring and Analogical Prototype Reasoning for E-commerce Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25683)
*Chen Yifei,Tian Zhixing,Wang Chenyang,Cheng Ziguang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: K-CARE框架通过外部知识增强LLM在电商搜索相关性任务中的能力，解决模型参数记忆中的领域知识缺失问题。核心包含对称上下文锚定(SCA)和类比原型推理(APR)两个组件，在真实电商平台验证中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于强化学习优化推理轨迹的方法无法解决LLM在电商搜索中的核心瓶颈——知识边界问题。当模型处理独特查询或细分产品时，参数记忆中的领域特定知识空白导致的上下文空白无法通过推理路径优化来弥补。

Method: K-CARE框架包含两个协同组件：(1) 对称上下文锚定(SCA)：通过行为衍生的隐式知识锚定查询和产品以填补上下文空白；(2) 类比原型推理(APR)：利用专家策划的原型知识通过上下文类比来校准决策边界。

Result: 在主流电商平台上的大规模离线评估和在线A/B测试表明，K-CARE显著优于现有最优基线方法，有效解决了知识密集型的相关性挑战。

Conclusion: 通过外部知识增强模型认知能力是突破LLM在工业场景中持续存在的"角落案例"性能瓶颈的有效路径，K-CARE为电商搜索场景提供了一种可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper targets e-commerce search relevance. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in this field, they often encounter performance bottlenecks in persistent 'corner cases' within complex industrial scenarios. Existing research primarily focuses on optimizing reasoning trajectories via Reinforcement Learning. However, real-world observations suggest that the primary bottleneck stems from knowledge boundaries, where the absence of domain-specific intelligence in the model's parametric memory creates a contextual void. This void persists when interpreting idiosyncratic queries or niche products and cannot be resolved solely through reasoning-path optimization.
  To bridge this gap, we propose K-CARE, a framework that extends the model's cognitive reach by grounding reasoning in external knowledge. K-CARE comprises two synergistic components: (1) Symmetrical Contextual Anchoring (SCA), which fills the contextual void by anchoring queries and products with behavior-derived implicit knowledge; and (2) Analogical Prototype Reasoning (APR), which leverages expert-curated prototypical knowledge to calibrate decision boundaries through in-context analogy. Extensive offline evaluations and online A/B tests on a leading e-commerce platform demonstrate that K-CARE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, delivering substantial commercial impact by resolving knowledge-intensive relevance challenges.

</details>


### [65] [From Citation Selection to Citation Absorption: A Measurement Framework for Generative Engine Optimization Across AI Search Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25707)
*Zhang Kai,Yao Jingang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究提出了生成式引擎优化（GEO）的两阶段测量框架，区分引用选择与引用吸收两个阶段。通过分析跨平台的 602 个提示和大规模数据集，发现不同生成式搜索引擎在引用广度和引用深度上存在显著差异，ChatGPT 引用更少但影响力更高，Perplexity 和 Google 则引用更广。该研究为 GEO 评估提供了超越引用计数的更精细视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式搜索引擎日益决定在线信息是否仅能被发现、被引用为来源，或真正被吸收进生成答案；现有研究缺乏对这两个关键阶段（引用选择和引用吸收）的系统测量方法。

Method: 采用两阶段测量框架：(1) 引用选择阶段，评估平台触发搜索并选取来源的过程；(2) 引用吸收阶段，评估被引用页面如何对最终答案的语言、证据、结构或事实支持做出贡献。数据集涵盖 602 个受控提示、21,143 条搜索层引用、23,745 条引用级特征记录、18,151 个成功获取页面及 72 个提取特征。

Result: ChatGPT 平均引用来源更少，但被获取页面中平均引用影响力显著更高；高影响力页面倾向更长、结构更丰富、语义对齐度更高，且富含可提取的证据（定义、数据、比较、步骤）。

Conclusion: GEO 应超越传统的引用数量指标，将答案层面的吸收（absorption）作为独立评估维度；高影响力页面通常更长、结构更清晰、语义对齐度更高，且富含可提取的证据（如定义、数据、比较、步骤）；不同生成式搜索引擎在引用策略上存在显著差异。

Abstract: Generative search engines increasingly determine whether online information is merely discoverable, cited as a source, or actually absorbed into generated answers. This paper proposes a two-stage measurement framework for Generative Engine Optimization (GEO): citation selection, where a platform triggers search and chooses sources, and citation absorption, where a cited page contributes language, evidence, structure, or factual support to the final answer. We analyze the public geo-citation-lab dataset covering 602 controlled prompts across ChatGPT, Google AI Overview/Gemini, and Perplexity; 21,143 valid search-layer citations; 23,745 citation-level feature records; 18,151 successfully fetched pages; and 72 extracted features. The central descriptive finding is that citation breadth and citation depth diverge. Perplexity and Google cite more sources on average, while ChatGPT cites fewer sources but shows substantially higher average citation influence among fetched pages. High-influence pages tend to be longer, more structured, semantically aligned, and richer in extractable evidence such as definitions, numerical facts, comparisons, and procedural steps. The results suggest that GEO should be measured beyond citation counts, with answer-level absorption treated as a separate outcome.

</details>


### [66] [Personalized Multi-Interest Modeling for Cross-Domain Recommendation to Cold-Start Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25732)
*Xiaodong Li,Jiawei Sheng,Jiangxia Cao,Xinghua Zhang,Wenyuan Zhang,Yong Sun,Shirui Pan,Zhihong Tian,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出NF-NPCDR框架，用于解决跨域推荐中冷启动用户的推荐问题。该框架通过个性化偏好编码器（融合归一化流与神经过程实现多模态偏好建模）、共同偏好编码器（利用偏好池捕捉用户间共性）和自适应解码器（动态融合两种偏好），克服了现有方法在个性化建模、共同偏好利用和多兴趣表示方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域推荐方法存在三个主要缺陷：基于嵌入与映射范式（EMCDR）的方法仅学习用户共享的映射函数，忽视了个性化偏好；基于元学习的改进方法虽然为每个用户学习独立映射函数，但忽略了用户间的共同偏好信息；此外，现有方法普遍将用户偏好压缩为单一整体表示，无法有效捕捉用户的多兴趣偏好。

Method: 首先设计了个性化偏好编码器，利用归一化流（NF）增强神经过程（NP），将高斯单模态分布转换为多模态分布，以建模用户的个性化多兴趣偏好；其次设计了包含偏好池的共同偏好编码器，捕捉不同用户间的共同偏好；最后引入随机自适应解码器，自适应地融合个性化和共同偏好进行推荐预测。

Result: 通过将归一化流与神经过程相结合，提供了一种捕捉用户个性化多兴趣偏好的新方法，能够将传统的单模态高斯分布转换为多模态分布，更好地建模用户复杂多样的兴趣特征。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于神经过程和归一化流的个性化多兴趣建模框架NF-NPCDR，有效解决了现有跨域推荐方法中用户个性化偏好建模不足和单一兴趣表示的局限。通过引入多模态分布建模和自适应解码机制，该方法能够同时捕捉用户的个性化多兴趣偏好和用户间的共同偏好，从而提升冷启动用户的推荐效果。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) has demonstrated to be an effective solution for alleviating the user cold-start issue. By leveraging rich user-item interactions available in a richly informative source domain, CDR could improve the recommendation performance for cold-start users in the target domain. Previous CDR approaches mostly adhere the Embedding and Mapping (EMCDR) paradigm, which learns a user-shared mapping function to transfer users' preference from the source domain to the target domain, neglecting users' personalized preference. Recent CDR approaches further leverage the meta-learning paradigm, considering the CDR task for each user independently and learning user-specific mapping functions for each user. However, they mostly learn representations for each user individually, which ignores the common preference between different users, neglecting valuable information for CDR. In addition, all these approaches usually summarize the user's preference into an overall representation, which can hardly capture the user's multi-interest preference. To this end, we propose a personalized multi-interest modeling framework for CDR to cold-start users, termed as NF-NPCDR. Specifically, we propose a personalized preference encoder that enhances the neural process (NP) with the normalizing flow (NF) to convert the Gaussian (unimodal) distribution to a multimodal distribution, providing a novel way to capture the user's personalized multi-interest preference. Then, we propose a common preference encoder with a preference pool to capture the common preference between different users. Furthermore, we introduce a stochastic adaptive decoder to incorporate both the personalized and common preference for cold-start users, adaptively modulating both preference for better recommendation.

</details>


### [67] [Harmonizing Generative Retrieval and Ranking in Chain-of-Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25787)
*Yu Liu,Jiangxia Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RecoChain提出统一生成式检索与排序框架，通过单一Transformer backbone同时实现候选生成和排序，解决现有生成式推荐系统在候选生成与排序性能之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式推荐系统（如OneRec）虽能通过语义ID生成候选项目，但在排序阶段存在性能差距——从beam-256生成的候选中筛选Top-10时难以准确评估项目优劣，导致生成能力与排序效果脱节。

Method: 采用统一Transformer架构：1）层级语义ID预测生成候选项目；2）基于SIM（Search-based Item Matching）的排序过程持续估计候选项目的点击概率。

Result: 在大规模真实数据集上验证，RecoChain有效弥合了生成检索与排序之间的性能差距，在保持强生成能力的同时显著提升了Top-K推荐性能。

Conclusion: 通过统一框架整合候选生成与排序两个阶段，RecoChain证明了生成式推荐系统中检索与排序协同优化的可行性，为下一代推荐系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative recommender systems have recently emerged as a promising paradigm by formulating next-item prediction as an auto-regressive semantic IDs generation, such as OneRec series works. However, with the next-item-agnostic prediction paradigm, its could beam out some next potential items via Semantic IDs but hard to estimate which items are better from them, e.g., select the top-10 from beam-256 items, leading to a gap between generation and ranking performance. To fulfill this gap, we propose RecoChain, a unified generative retrieval and ranking framework that integrates candidate generation and ranking within a single Transformer backbone. Specifically, in inference, the model first generates candidate items via hierarchical semantic ID prediction, then performs the SIM-based ranking process to estimate the click possibility of corresponding item candidate continuously. Extensive experiments on large-scale real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach effectively bridges the gap between generative retrieval and ranking, achieving improved Top-K recommendation performance while maintaining strong generative capability.

</details>


### [68] [Break the Inaccessible Boundary: Distilling Post-Conversion Content for User Retention Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25839)
*Tianbao Ma,Ruochen Yang,Chengen Li,Yuexin Shi,Jiangxia Cao,Linxun Chen,Zhaojie Liu,Yanan Niu,Han Li,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出OCARM框架，通过两阶段蒸馏对齐方法解决RTB广告系统中保留率建模的特征泄露问题，使模型能够在推理阶段隐式利用onboarding content信息而无需直接访问该信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RTB广告系统的用户再参与场景中，保留率模型需要在用户转化前出价时预测其未来回访概率。虽然onboarding content（转化后内容）提供了高度信息化的保留率预测信号，但直接使用会造成严重特征泄露，导致训练与推理阶段存在差距。

Method: OCARM采用两阶段蒸馏对齐框架。第一阶段：将onboarding content作为输入训练分层编码器，生成教师表征。第二阶段：冻结教师编码器，通过蒸馏将用户编码器与教师表征对齐，使模型在不泄露的情况下近似获取不可访问的onboarding信号。

Result: 离线实验和在线A/B测试均表明，该框架在实际增长场景中实现了一致的改进。

Conclusion: OCARM通过两阶段蒸馏对齐框架，成功解决了保留率建模中的特征泄露问题，使模型能够在推理时隐式捕获未来内容信号，显著提升了用户保留率预测的准确性。

Abstract: User retention is a key metric to measure long-term engagement in modern platforms. In real-time bidding (RTB) advertising system for user re-engagement, the retention model is required to predict future revisit probability at bidding time, before the user converts and consumes any content. Although post-conversion content, termed Onboarding Content, provides highly informative signals for retention prediction, directly using it in training causes severe feature leakage and creates a gap between training and serving. To address this issue, we propose OCARM, a two-stage distillation-aligned framework for Onboarding Content Augmented Retention Modeling, enabling the model to implicitly capture future content using only observable features during inference. In the first stage, we deliberately expose onboarding content to train a hierarchical encoder that produces teacher representations. In the second stage, a user encoder is aligned with the frozen teacher through distillation, allowing the model to approximate the inaccessible onboarding signals without leakage. Extensive offline experiments and online A/B tests demonstrate that our framework achieves consistent improvements in a real-world growth scenario.

</details>


### [69] [Make Any Collection Navigable: Methods for Constructing and Evaluating Hypergraph of Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25906)
*Dean E. Alvarez,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究了如何构建可导航的文本超图（HoT），提出了多种构建方法和一个新的评估指标effort ratio，发现简单的TF-IDF方法就能达到与LLM方法相当的导航结构质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页的超链接结构虽然支持灵活导航，但手动创建的超链接无法完全捕获语料库中隐含的语义结构，因此需要一种通用的方法使任意集合可导航。

Method: 提出了多种HoT构建方法，并设计了新的量化评估指标——effort ratio来评估HoT的结构质量。

Result: 实验结果表明，简单的TF-IDF基线方法在effort ratio指标上可以与基于LLM的方法相媲美。

Conclusion: 简单的TF-IDF基线方法在提出的effort ratio指标上能够匹配基于LLM的方法，表明该领域可能不需要复杂的语言模型即可达到良好的导航结构质量。

Abstract: One reason the Web is more useful than a simple collection of documents is that the structure created by hyperlinks enables flexible navigation from one web page to another. However, hyperlinks are typically created manually and cannot fully capture a corpus' implicit semantic structures. Is there a general way to make an arbitrary collection navigable? Recent work has formalized this problem generally as constructing a Hypergraph of Text (HoT), which provides a formal mathematical structure for supporting navigation and browsing. However, how to construct and evaluate a Hypergraph of Text remains a challenge. In this paper, we propose and study several methods for constructing a HoT. We also propose a novel quantitative metric, effort ratio, for evaluating the structural quality of a constructed HoT. Experimental results show that even simple TF-IDF baselines can match LLM-based methods on our proposed effort ratio metric.

</details>

### [70] [On the Average-Case Performance of Greedy for Maximum Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24884)
*Eric Balkanski,Jason Chatzitheodorou,Flore Sentenac*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文在左正则随机模型下分析最大覆盖问题贪心算法的平均case性能，证明其期望逼近比总优于最坏情况的 1-1/e，并在特定条件下接近 1，同时发现存在逼近比不超过 0.94 的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最大覆盖问题中，贪心算法的最坏情况逼近比为 1-1/e，实证评估表明在真实数据上逼近比接近 1，但缺乏平均case理论解释。

Method: 采用左正则随机模型分析贪心算法；开发了微分方程方法的新应用，并建立与 Erdős-Rényi 图最大匹配的联系。

Result: 在所有参数设置下，贪心算法的期望逼近比比最坏情况保证提高了一个常数；当满足两个简单条件之一时，期望逼近比接近 1；但存在一个 regime 下贪心算法的期望逼近比不超过 0.94。

Conclusion: 提出了新的分析工具，包括微分方程方法的创新应用和与 Erdős-Rényi 图最大匹配的联系，这些结果对其他随机模型也具有参考价值。

Abstract: For the classical maximum coverage problem, the greedy algorithm achieves a worst-case $1-1/e$ approximation, which is optimal unless $\text{P} = \text{NP}$. The notion of coverage appears in a wide range of optimization tasks, where empirical evaluations indicate approximation ratios close to $1$ for the greedy algorithm on real data. Random models have provided average-case justifications for the empirical performance of many well-known algorithms, but little is known about the average-case performance of greedy for maximum coverage. We analyze the expected approximation ratio of the greedy algorithm in a random model, which we call the left-regular random model. We first show that, for all parameter settings of this model, the expected approximation ratio of the greedy algorithm improves by a constant over its worst-case $1-1/e$ guarantee. We then identify two simple conditions, either of which ensures that the expected approximation ratio is close to $1$ for sufficiently large graphs. Finally, we show that there is a regime where greedy does not achieve an expected approximation better than $0.94$. To obtain these results, we develop analytical tools, including a novel application of the differential equation method and a connection to maximum matching in Erdős-Rényi graphs, which may be of independent interest for other random models.

</details>


### [71] [An Efficient Streaming Algorithm for Approximating Graphlet Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25400)
*Marco Bressan,T-H. Hubert Chan,Qipeng Kuang,Mauro Sozio*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In recent years, the problem of computing the frequencies of the induced $k$-vertex subgraphs of a graph, or \emph{$k$-graphlets}, has become central. One approach for this problem is to sample $k$-graphlets randomly. Classic algorithms for $k$-graphlet sampling require loading the entire graph into main memory, making them impractical for massive graphs. To bypass this limitation, Bourreau et al. (NeurIPS 2024) introduced a \emph{streaming} algorithm that through nontrivial techniques makes only $O(\log n)$ passes using $O(n \log n)$ memory. In this work we break their $O(\log n)$-pass bound by giving an algorithm that, for any fixed $c>0$, makes $O(1/c)$ passes using $\tilde O(n^{1+c})$ memory. As a consequence of their lower bound, our algorithm is optimal up to a factor of $\tilde{O}(n^c)$ in the memory usage. We use this sampling algorithm to obtain an efficient method of approximating $k$-graphlet distributions. Experiments on real-world and synthetic graphs show that our algorithm is always at least as good as the one of Bourreau et al., and outperforms it by orders of magnitude on mildly dense graphs.

</details>


### [72] [Two Efficient Message-passing Exclusive Scan Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25667)
*Jesper Larsson Träff*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究消息传递系统中并行扫描原语（parallel scan primitives）的exclusive前缀和计算问题。在有界单端口通信能力下，至少需要⌈log₂p⌉或⌈log₂(p-1)⌉次发送-接收通信轮次。文章提出了两种算法：第一种结合inclusive和exclusive扫描阶段，在通信轮数和操作符应用次数之间进行权衡；第二种是对轮最优all-reduce算法的改进，额外操作符应用次数取决于p-1的比特位计数。这两种算法适用于输入向量较小、通信轮次主导性能的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Exclusive scan问题在并行计算中与inclusive scan同样重要，但相关研究较少。消息传递系统中，p个连续排名的处理器需要在结合运算下计算前缀和，现有inclusive scan算法简单高效，但exclusive scan缺乏高效解决方案。

Method: 1. 引入exclusive前缀和问题的自然不变量（natural invariants）进行分析；2. 提出第一种算法：包含inclusive扫描阶段和exclusive扫描阶段，通过增加操作符应用次数来减少通信轮次；3. 提出第二种算法：修改轮最优all-reduce算法，额外操作符应用次数取决于p-1的popcount。

Result: 第一种算法：总通信轮次q=⌈log₂p⌉，最小inclusive扫描轮次q'≥q-log₂(2^q-p+1)，实现了通信轮次与操作符应用的权衡；第二种算法：通过修改all-reduce实现round-optimal性能，额外操作符开销与p-1比特位设置数相关。

Conclusion: 对于输入向量较小、通信轮次主导性能的场景，两种算法均有效；对于大输入向量，需使用流水线或固定度树算法。

Abstract: Parallel scan primitives compute element-wise inclusive or exclusive prefix sums of input vectors contributed by $p$ consecutively ranked processors under an associative, possibly expensive, binary operator $\oplus$. In message-passing systems with bounded, one-ported communication capabilities, at least $\lceil\log_2 p\rceil$ or $\lceil\log_2 (p-1)\rceil$ send-receive communication rounds are required to perform the scans. While there are well-known, simple algorithms for the inclusive scan that solve the problem in $\lceil\log_2 p\rceil$ send-receive communication rounds with $\lceil\log_2 p\rceil$ applications of the $\oplus$ operator, the exclusive scan is different and has been much less addressed.
  By considering natural invariants for the exclusive prefix sums problem, we present two different algorithms that are efficient in the number of communication rounds and in the number of applications of the $\oplus$ operator. The first algorithm consists of an inclusive scan phase and an exclusive scan phase and trades the number of communication rounds against the number of applications of the $\oplus$ operator. The smallest number of inclusive scan rounds with $q=\lceil\log_2 p\rceil$ rounds in total is $q'\geq q-\log_2(2^q-p+1)$. The other algorithm is a modification of a round-optimal all-reduce algorithm, and the number of additional applications of the $\oplus$ operator is dependent on the number of bits set (popcount of) in $p-1$. Both algorithms are relevant for small(er) input vectors where performance is dominated by the number of communication rounds. For large input vectors, other (pipelined, fixed-degree tree) algorithms must be used.

</details>


### [73] [SimdQuickHeap: The QuickHeap Reconsidered](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25681)
*Johannes Breitling,Ragnar Groot Koerkamp,Marvin Williams*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出SimdQuickHeap，一种利用SIMD指令优化的优先级队列数据结构，通过将所有pivot相邻存储在内存中并将元素存储在专用bucket中，实现比传统隐式二叉堆更好的缓存局部性和SIMD并行性。在综合基准测试中比包括非比较基数堆在内的竞争对手快最多2倍，在Dijkstra最短路径和Jarnik-Prim最小生成树算法中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统隐式二叉堆作为最广泛使用的优先级队列，存在频繁的缓存未命中问题，且难以利用SIMD等向量化指令进行优化。原始QuickHeap虽有一定优势，但在现代CPU架构上仍有改进空间。

Method: 1) 修改原始QuickHeap的数据布局，使所有pivot在内存中相邻存储；2) 将元素存储在专用bucket中；3) 利用SIMD指令实现bucket的高效分区和pivot列表的快速扫描；4) 复杂度分析采用摊销期望分析方法。

Result: 摊销期望时间复杂度O(log n)，在非退化情况下可达O(1/W log n)，其中W为SIMD寄存器字数；I/O复杂度：push为O(1/B)，pop为O(1/B log₂(n/M))。综合基准测试显示比最佳竞争对手快最多2倍，每对push和pop操作约需1.5log₂n次比较和log₂n纳秒。

Conclusion: SimdQuickHeap通过创新的数据布局和SIMD并行化，在理论和实践层面均显著优于现有优先级队列实现，在图算法（Dijkstra和Jarnik-Prim）中表现持续最优，具有重要的工程应用价值。

Abstract: Priority queues are data structures that maintain a dynamic collection of elements and allow inserting new elements and removing the smallest element. The most widely known and used priority queue is likely the implicit binary heap, even though it is has frequent cache misses and is hard to optimize using e.g. SIMD instructions.
  We introduce the SimdQuickHeap, a variant of the QuickHeap that was introduced by Navarro and Paredes in 2010. As suggested by the name, the data structure bears some similarity to QuickSort. We modify the data layout of the original QuickHeap to have all \emph{pivots} adjacent in memory, with elements between consecutive pivots stored in dedicated \emph{buckets}. This allows efficient SIMD implementations for both partitioning of buckets and scanning the list of pivots to find the bucket to append newly inserted elements to. The SimdQuickHeap has amortized expected complexity $O(\log n)$ per operation, which improves to $O(\frac 1W\log n)$ in non-degenerate cases, where $W$ is the number of words in a SIMD register. In this case, the I/O-complexity is amortized $O(\frac 1B)$ per push and $O(\frac 1B \log_2 \frac nM)$ per pop.
  In synthetic benchmarks, the SimdQuickHeap is up to twice as fast as the next-best competitor, including the non-comparison radix heap, and needs around $1.5\log_2 n$ comparisons and $\log_2 n$ nanoseconds per pair of push and pop operations. On graph benchmarks with Dijkstra's shortest path algorithm and Jarnik-Prim's minimum spanning tree algorithm, the SimdQuickHeap is consistently the fastest.

</details>


### [74] [Clustering Permutations under the Ulam Metric: A Parameterized Complexity Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25734)
*Tian Bai,Fedor V. Fomin,Petr A. Golovach,Yash Hiren More,Simon Wietheger*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Ulam度量下排序聚合问题的参数化复杂性。主要贡献包括：Ulam k-center问题在d=1时仍为NP难，但关于k+d是固定参数可解的（FPT），且不存在多项式核；Ulam k-median问题关于总距离d是W[1]-难的，但存在XP算法和关于k+d的多项式核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序聚合在社会选择、信息检索和计算生物学等领域有重要应用。虽然Kendall tau距离下的问题已有充分研究，但基于编辑距离的Ulam度量下的参数化复杂性尚未被系统探索。

Method: 采用参数化算法设计方法，包括：针对Ulam k-center问题设计了基于局部搜索的FPT算法框架；使用核下界归约技术证明多项式核不存在；通过从Set Cover等问题的归约证明W[1]-硬度。

Result: 1) Ulam k-center在d=1时NP难，但关于(k,d)是FPT的，且不存在多项式核（除非NP⊆coNP/poly）；2) Ulam k-median关于d是W[1]-难的，但存在XP算法；3) Ulam k-median关于k+d存在多项式核，从而也是FPT的。

Conclusion: Ulam k-center和k-median问题在参数化复杂性上呈现有趣的对比：前者对d的少量增加即可实现FPT，后者则对d本身是W[1]-难的，但两者对(k+d)参数化都是FPT的。研究揭示了Ulam距离非局部特性带来的算法设计挑战。

Abstract: Rank aggregation seeks a representative permutation for a collection of rankings and plays a central role in areas such as social choice, information retrieval, and computational biology. Two fundamental aggregation tasks are the center and median problems, which minimize the maximum and the total distance to the input permutations, respectively. While these problems are well understood under Kendall's tau and related distances, their parameterized complexity under the Ulam metric, an edit-distance-based metric on permutations, has remained largely unexplored.
  In this work, we initiate a systematic study of the parameterized complexity of rank aggregation under the Ulam metric. We consider both the center and median problems, as well as their generalizations to the $k$-center and $k$-median clustering settings, parameterized by the number of centers $k$ and the distance budget $d$ (corresponding to the maximum distance for center variants and the total distance for median variants). Both problems are known to be NP-hard already for $k=1$.
  We show that the Ulam $k$-center problem remains NP-hard when $d=1$, but is fixed-parameter tractable when parameterized by $k + d$. Our algorithm is based on a novel local-search framework tailored to the non-local nature of Ulam distances. We complement this by proving that no polynomial kernel exists for the $k+d$ parameterization unless NP $\subseteq$ coNP/poly. For the Ulam $k$-median problem parameterized by the total distance $d$, we establish W[1]-hardness and provide an XP algorithm. We also provide a polynomial kernel for the parameter $k + d$, which in turn yields a fixed-parameter tractable algorithm.

</details>


### [75] [Tight Bounds for some W[1]-hard Problems Parameterized by Multi-clique-width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25841)
*Benjamin Bergougnoux,Vera Chekan,Stefan Kratsch*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了参数化为multi-clique-width的难题的细粒度复杂度。证明了Max Cut在multi-clique-width下存在紧的下界n^{2^{o(k)}}，这与clique-width下的已知上界n^{O(k)}形成差异，使其成为首个显示两种参数化具有不同最优运行时间的难题。同时证明Hamiltonian Cycle和Edge Dominating Set在这两种参数化下具有相同的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究multi-clique-width参数的细粒度复杂度理论。虽然multi-clique-width在功能上与clique-width等价（可以相互界定），但两者的实际运行时间可能存在差异。该研究旨在探索是否存在对这两个参数具有不同最优运行时间的难题，以及能否建立更细粒度的复杂度下界。

Method: 论文使用 ETH（指数时间假设）进行下界约归证明。具体而言，通过构造从已知难问题到所研究问题的约归来证明Max Cut在multi-clique-width参数下不存在亚指数双指数时间算法。在正面结果方面，设计了时间复杂度为 n^{O(k)} 的精确算法来处理 Hamiltonian Cycle 和 Edge Dominating Set。

Result: 1. 在ETH下，Max Cut在multi-clique-width为k的图上不存在时间复杂度为n^{2^{o(k)}}·f(k)的算法（对任意可计算函数f）。而在clique-width参数下，已知存在紧致的n^{O(k)}算法（Fomin et al., 2014）。2. Hamiltonian Cycle和Edge Dominating Set在multi-clique-width k的图上可以在时间n^{O(k)}内解决，与clique-width的紧致运行时间相匹配。

Conclusion: Max Cut成为第一个已知对多 clique-width 和 clique-width 参数化具有不同紧致运行时间的难题，同时回答了 Chekan 和 Kratsch（MFCS 2023）提出的三个开放问题，丰富了形式为 n^{2^{o(k)}}·f(k) 的下界列表。

Abstract: In this work we contribute to the study of the fine-grained complexity of problems parameterized by multi-clique-width, which was initiated by Fürer [ITCS 2017] and pursued further by Chekan and Kratsch [MFCS 2023]. Multi-clique-width is a parameter defined analogously to clique-width but every vertex is allowed to hold multiple labels simultaneously. This parameter is upper-bounded by both clique-width and treewidth (plus a constant), hence it generalizes both of them without an exponential blow-up. Conversely, graphs of multi-clique-width $k$ have clique-width at most $2^k$, and there exist graphs with clique-width at least $2^{Ω(k)}$. Thus, while the two parameters are functionally equivalent, the fine-grained complexity of problems may differ relative to them.
  As our first and main result we show that under ETH the Max Cut problem cannot be solved in time $n^{2^{o(k)}} \cdot f(k)$ on graphs of multi-clique-width $k$ for any computable function $f$. For clique-width $k$ an $n^{\mathcal{O}(k)}$ algorithm by Fomin et al. [SIAM J. Comput. 2014] is tight under ETH. This makes Max Cut the first known problem for which the tight running times differ for parameterization by clique-width and multi-clique-width and it contributes to the short list of known lower bounds of form $n^{2^{o(k)}} \cdot f(k)$. As our second contribution we show that Hamiltonian Cycle and Edge Dominating Set can be solved in time $n^{\mathcal{O}(k)}$ on graphs of multi-clique-width $k$ matching the tight running time for clique-width. These results answer three questions left open by Chekan and Kratsch [MFCS 2023].

</details>

### [76] [VisualNeo: Bridging the Gap between Visual Query Interfaces and Graph Query Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25283)
*Kai Huang,Houdong Liang,Chongchong Yao,Xi Zhao,Yue Cui,Yao Tian,Ruiyuan Zhang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出VisualNeo，一个基于Neo4j的可视化图查询界面(VQI)系统，旨在弥合可视化查询构造与底层图查询引擎之间的技术鸿沟，使非程序员能够高效地进行子图查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可视图查询界面(VQI)和图查询引擎(GQE)两个领域传统上相互独立，导致非专业用户难以完成从查询构造到结果探索的完整图查询流程，用户体验存在断层。

Method: VisualNeo继承自近期先进VQI系统的数据驱动GUI设计和预置模式生成功能，同时集成数据库管理器模块以连接通用Neo4j数据库，通过Neo4j驱动执行查询处理，并提供美观的查询结果展示界面。

Result: 该系统实现了在大型图数据库中进行高效子图查询的能力，同时保持了用户友好的可视化交互体验和专业的后端查询处理能力。

Conclusion: VisualNeo通过整合前端可视化查询与后端图数据库引擎，为用户提供了一套完整的全栈式图查询解决方案，填补了VQI与GQE之间的技术割裂。

Abstract: Visual Graph Query Interfaces (VQIs) empower non-programmers to query graph data by constructing visual queries intuitively. Devising efficient technologies in Graph Query Engines (GQEs) for interactive search and exploration has also been studied for years. However, these two vibrant scientific fields are traditionally independent of each other, causing a vast barrier for users who wish to explore the full-stack operations of graph querying. In this demonstration, we propose a novel VQI system built upon Neo4j called VisualNeo that facilities an efficient subgraph query in large graph databases. VisualNeo inherits several advanced features from recent advanced VQIs, which include the data-driven gui design and canned pattern generation. Additionally, it embodies a database manager module in order that users can connect to generic Neo4j databases. It performs query processing through the Neo4j driver and provides an aesthetic query result exploration.

</details>

### [77] [ITAS: A Multi-Agent Architecture for LLM-Based Intelligent Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24808)
*Iizalaarab Elhaimeur,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Abstract: Large language model tutors are easy to build in a notebook and hard to run in a real course. We describe ITAS (Intelligent Teaching Assistant System), a multi-agent tutoring system that a graduate quantum computing course used for a semester at Old Dominion University. The system has three layers. The teaching layer is a Spoke-and-Wheel of three parallel specialist agents (Video, Code, Guidance) followed by a Synthesizer, plus a separate autograder that evaluates both the correctness and the approach of checkpoint submissions. The operational layer is four Cloud Run microservices with session state in Cloud SQL and interaction events streamed through Pub/Sub to BigQuery. The feedback layer is a narrow-scope conversational agent that answers instructor questions over per-lesson pseudonymized event streams, addressing what we call the Blind Instructor Problem: LLM tutors accumulate more data about students than the instructor can reach through routine channels. The architecture is a direct response to specific failures of an earlier prototype, and we describe which of those fixes carried forward and which were dropped for this iteration. We report on a pilot deployment (five students, one course, one semester) interpreted as system-behavior evidence rather than learning-outcome evidence: the teaching layer handled 334 chat turns without the task-boundary hallucinations that domain consolidation would have risked, the operational layer captured 10,628 events across five modules, and the feedback layer surfaced two findings the instructor acted on mid-semester. We do not claim the pilot generalizes. We do claim that the system as described is one workable answer to the question of what an LLM-based ITS needs to look like end-to-end to run in a real course.

</details>


### [78] [MultiHedge: Adaptive Coordination via Retrieval-Augmented Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24905)
*Feliks Bańka,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: MultiHedge是一种混合架构，通过检索历史先例增强LLM决策能力，在美股市场评估中证明记忆增强检索比单纯增加模型规模更能提升系统鲁棒性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在应对变化环境和不确定性时常常失败，难以在动态变化的条件下进行泛化，且表现出不稳定行为。

Method: 提出MultiHedge混合架构：LLM基于检索到的历史先例生成结构化分配决策，执行层基于标准期权策略。使用美股市场进行受控评估，与基于规则和基于学习的基线方法进行比较。

Result: 记忆增强检索比单纯增加模型规模能够带来更好的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 记忆和架构设计在模块化决策系统中扮演核心角色，对系统鲁棒性至关重要。

Abstract: Decision-making under changing conditions remains a fundamental challenge in many real-world systems. Existing approaches often fail to generalize across shifting regimes and exhibit unstable behavior under uncertainty. This raises the research question: can retrieval-augmented LLM coordination improve the robustness of modular decision pipelines? We propose MultiHedge, a hybrid architecture where an LLM produces structured allocation decisions conditioned on retrieved historical precedents, and execution is grounded in canonical option strategies. In a controlled evaluation using U.S. equities, we compare MultiHedge to rule-based and learning-based baselines. The key result is that memory-augmented retrieval confers greater robustness and stability than increasing model scale alone. Our paper contributes a controlled computational study showing that memory and architectural design play a central role in robustness in modular decision systems.

</details>


### [79] [Frontier Coding Agents Can Now Implement an AlphaZero Self-Play Machine Learning Pipeline For Connect Four That Performs Comparably to an External Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25067)
*Joshua Sherwood,Ben Aybar,Benjamin Kaplan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Forecasting when AI systems will become capable of meaningfully accelerating AI research is a central challenge for AI safety. Existing benchmarks measure broad capability growth, but may not provide ample early warning signals for recursive self-improvement. We propose measuring AI's capability to autonomously implement end-to-end machine learning pipelines from past AI research breakthroughs, given a minimal task description. By providing a concise task description instead of the full prior work as reference, we hope to better elicit emerging AI research taste. We introduce a proof-of-concept benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously implement an AlphaZero-style machine learning pipeline for Connect Four on consumer hardware within a three-hour budget, and we evaluate the resulting game AIs in a round-robin tournament anchored to the Pascal Pons Connect Four solver. Across four agents with eight trials each, we find substantial differentiation: Claude Opus 4.7 won as first-mover against Pons in seven of eight trials, statistically significantly better than the other agents tested, none of which exceeded two of eight. The task, which no frontier agent could reliably complete when we began development in January of 2026, is now near-saturation. Our evaluation also surfaced anomalous behavior in GPT-5.4, which consistently used far less of its allocated time budget than other agents. A follow-up 16-trial probe using shorter, less evaluation-coded prompts substantially increased GPT-5.4's time-budget usage, consistent with but not diagnostic of sandbagging; Bradley-Terry ratings across probe conditions showed only directional differences, despite significant differences in time-budget usage. We release our data, code, and prompts to support reproduction and extension.

</details>


### [80] [Where Did It Go Wrong? Capability-Oriented Failure Attribution for Vision-and-Language Navigation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25161)
*Jianming Chen,Yawen Wang,Junjie Wang,Xiaofei Xie,Shoubin Li,Qing Wang,Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向能力的测试方法，通过自适应测试用例生成、专用能力预言器和反馈机制，实现对具身智能体能力缺陷的精准检测与归因，为系统改进提供更清晰的指导方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体在视觉-语言导航等安全关键应用中依赖多个相互依赖的能力（感知、记忆、规划、决策），现有系统级测试方法难以定位和归因具体能力缺陷，导致改进方向不明确。

Method: 提出了一种面向能力的测试方法，融合三个核心组件：1）通过种子选择和变异实现自适应测试用例生成；2）用于识别特定能力错误的能力预言器（capability oracles）；3）将失败归因于具体能力并指导后续测试生成的反馈机制。

Result: 该方法在失败案例发现数量和能力级缺陷识别准确性上均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 实验表明，该方法比现有基线方法能够发现更多失败案例、更准确地识别能力级别的缺陷，提供更可解释和可操作的改进指导。

Abstract: Embodied agents in safety-critical applications such as Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) rely on multiple interdependent capabilities (e.g., perception, memory, planning, decision), making failures difficult to localize and attribute. Existing testing methods are largely system-level and provide limited insight into which capability deficiencies cause task failures. We propose a capability-oriented testing approach that enables failure detection and attribution by combining (1) adaptive test case generation via seed selection and mutation, (2) capability oracles for identifying capability-specific errors, and (3) a feedback mechanism that attributes failures to capabilities and guides further test generation. Experiments show that our method discovers more failure cases and more accurately pinpoints capability-level deficiencies than state-of-the-art baselines, providing more interpretable and actionable guidance for improving embodied agents.

</details>


### [81] [Pythia: Toward Predictability-Driven Agent-Native LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25899)
*Shan Yu,Junyi Shu,Yuanjiang Ni,Kun Qian,Xue Li,Yang Wang,Jinyuan Zhang,Ziyi Xu,Shuo Yang,Lingjun Zhu,Ennan Zhai,Qingda Lu,Jiarong Xing,Youyou Lu,Xin Jin,Xuanzhe Liu,Harry Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体架构的服务优化问题，分析了生产环境中的性能瓶颈，并提出Pythia系统。该系统通过捕获工作流语义来解锁优化机会，在吞吐量和任务完成时间上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在处理多智能体架构时将其视为通用流量，未能利用多智能体工作负载的结构化特性（如约束智能体行为和暴露语义可预测性），导致前缀缓存命中率低、长上下文请求的资源竞争严重、次优扩展引发的排队延迟等问题。

Method: 设计了一个多智能体服务系统Pythia，通过简单的接口在服务层捕获工作流语义，从而解锁新的优化机会。

Result: 相比现有最优基线，Pythia显著提升了吞吐量（throughput）和任务完成时间（job completion time）。

Conclusion: Pythia通过在服务层捕获工作流语义，为多智能体系统提供了显著的性能提升，相比现有基线大幅改善了吞吐量和任务完成时间。

Abstract: As LLM applications grow more complex, developers are increasingly adopting multi-agent architectures to decompose workflows into specialized, collaborative components, introducing structure that constrains agent behavior and exposes useful semantic predictability. Unlike traditional LLM serving, which operates under highly dynamic and uncertain conditions, this structured topology enables opportunities to reduce runtime uncertainty -- yet existing systems fail to exploit it, treating agentic workloads as generic traffic and incurring significant inefficiencies. Our analysis of production traces from an agent-serving platform and an internal coding assistant reveals key bottlenecks, including low prefix cache hit rates, severe resource contention from long-context requests, and substantial queuing delays due to suboptimal scaling. To address these challenges, we propose Pythia, a multi-agent serving system that captures workflow semantics through a simple interface at the serving layer, unlocking new optimization opportunities and substantially improving throughput and job completion time over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [From Prototype to Classroom: An Intelligent Tutoring System for Quantum Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24807)
*Iizalaarab Elhaimeur,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究提出了ITAS（智能教学助手系统），通过五模块量子课程、辐条-轮毂专业化智能体架构、生产级云基础设施和会话分析层四部分构建多智能体辅导系统。在东弗吉尼亚大学的量子计算课程中试点验证，结果表明智能体专业化可解决量子教育的可靠性问题，系统可在真实课堂规模部署，且能为教师提供隐藏的课程缺口洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算教育面临三重困境：概念反直觉、数学形式化密集、合格师资严重匮乏于资源有限的机构。现有的知识图谱增强辅导系统仅在模拟环境中验证，未能回答专业化智能体能否处理真实课堂负荷、是否能生产部署、以及能否为教师提供可操作情报等关键问题。

Method: ITAS采用辐条-轮毂（Spoke-and-Wheel）教学架构，将教学任务分配给量子领域专业化的多个LLM智能体；基于Watrous的"信息优先"框架设计五模块量子课程体系；构建符合生产环境要求且满足监管合规的云基础设施；部署会话分析层以实时监测教学交互并生成课程洞察。采用准实验设计在真实课堂环境中进行试点评估。

Result: 试点教学验证了三个核心问题：(1) 智能体专业化有效解决了原型系统中观察到的任务边界失效问题；(2) 云基础设施以低于教科书的成本支持课堂规模并发访问；(3) 分析智能体发现了教师难以自主察觉的课程缺口，提供了可操作的改进情报。

Conclusion: ITAS证明了在量子信息科学等高技术要求领域，多智能体专业化分工能够有效解决任务边界失效问题；云基础设施可支持课堂规模并发且成本可控；分析智能体能够发现课程设计中的盲点。这项工作为AI辅助量子教育从实验室演示走向真实课堂提供了可复制的技术路线。

Abstract: Quantum computing instructors face a compounding problem: the concepts are counterintuitive, the mathematical formalism is dense, and qualified faculty are scarce outside a small number of well-resourced institutions. Our prior work introduced a knowledge-graph-augmented tutoring prototype with two specialized LLM agents: a Teaching Agent for dynamic interaction and a Lesson Planning Agent for lesson generation. Validated on simulated runs rather than in a real course, that prototype left open whether more aggressive agent specialization would be needed to handle the full range of quantum education tasks under real student load. This paper answers the three questions that the prototype could not answer. Can agent specialization solve the reliability problem in a domain as technically demanding as quantum information science? Can the system run in a real course, not a demonstration? Does the instructor gain actionable intelligence from the deployment? We present ITAS (Intelligent Teaching Assistant System), a multi-agent tutoring system built around four contributions: a five-module QIS curriculum grounded in Watrous's information-first framework, a Spoke-and-Wheel teaching architecture with quantum-specialized agents, a cloud infrastructure designed for production use and regulatory compliance, and a conversational analytics layer for instructors and content developers. Piloted in a quantum computing course at Old Dominion University, the system supports all three answers: deployment evidence is consistent with specialization addressing the task-boundary failures observed in the prototype, cloud infrastructure supports classroom-scale concurrency at sub-textbook cost, and the analytics agent surfaces curriculum gaps the instructor could not otherwise see.

</details>


### [83] [Safety Drift After Fine-Tuning: Evidence from High-Stakes Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24902)
*Emaan Bilal Khan,Amy Winecoff,Miranda Bogen,Dylan Hadfield-Menell*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究发现微调后模型的安全行为表现出高度不稳定性和不一致性，表明当前依赖基础模型评估来管理下游风险的做法存在重大缺陷，亟需在部署相关情境下对微调模型进行重新评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型的微调通常在特定领域应用，而安全评估通常仅在基础模型上进行，隐含假设安全属性会通过下游适应持续存在。该研究旨在验证这一假设是否成立。

Method: 分析了100个模型（包括医学和法律领域的广泛部署的微调模型，以及开放基础模型及其基线的受控适应版本），使用通用和领域特定的安全基准进行评估。

Result: 良性的微调会诱导大规模、异质性且常常矛盾的安全测量变化：模型常在某些评估指标上改善，同时在另一些指标上退化，不同评估之间存在实质性分歧。

Conclusion: 安全行为在下游适应过程中并不稳定，当前基于基础模型评估的做法不足以充分管理下游风险，特别是在高风险场景中。这对基于基础模型评估的治理和部署实践提出了严峻挑战，需要在部署相关情境中对微调模型进行明确的重新评估。

Abstract: Foundation models are routinely fine-tuned for use in particular domains, yet safety assessments are typically conducted only on base models, implicitly assuming that safety properties persist through downstream adaptation. We test this assumption by analyzing the safety behavior of 100 models, including widely deployed fine-tunes in the medical and legal domains as well as controlled adaptations of open foundation models alongside their bases. Across general-purpose and domain-specific safety benchmarks, we find that benign fine-tuning induces large, heterogeneous, and often contradictory changes in measured safety: models frequently improve on some instruments while degrading on others, with substantial disagreement across evaluations. These results show that safety behavior is not stable under ordinary downstream adaptation, raising critical questions about governance and deployment practices centered on base-model evaluations. Without explicit re-evaluation of fine-tuned models in deployment-relevant contexts, such approaches fall short of adequately managing downstream risk, overlooking practical sources of harm -- failures that are especially consequential in high-stakes settings and challenge current accountability paradigms.

</details>


### [84] [Risk Reporting for Developers' Internal AI Model Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24966)
*Oscar Delaney,Sambhav Maheshwari,Joe O'Brien,Theo Bearman,Oliver Guest*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文为前沿AI开发者提供了统一的内部使用风险报告框架，涵盖自主AI不当行为和内部人员威胁两大威胁向量及手段、动机、机会三要素，旨在满足加州SB 53、纽约RAISE Act和欧盟GPAI行为准则三大监管要求，填补了现有AI安全监管框架的统一报告标准空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI公司通常将最先进的模型部署于内部进行安全测试和迭代，但这种内部使用可能产生外部部署框架无法解决的风险。现有法律框架（加州SB 53、纽约RAISE Act、欧盟通用目的AI行为准则）均要求前沿开发者制定内部使用风险管理计划并生成相关风险报告，但缺乏统一的报告标准。

Method: 本文提出了一种统一的报告框架，结构化为两个威胁向量——自主AI不当行为和内部人员威胁，每个向量包含三个风险因素：手段（means）、动机（motive）和机会（opportunity）。该框架旨在使公司能够生成符合多个监管框架要求的高质量内部使用风险报告。

Result: 本文提供了一份适用于加州SB 53、纽约RAISE Act和欧盟GPAI行为准则三大监管框架的内部使用风险报告统一标准，定义了良好报告的关键要素，为评估和安全团队提供具体指导，同时为监管机构和审计人员提供评判依据。

Conclusion: 本文提出的报告框架可能是确保内部AI使用风险在显现前得到识别的少数机制之一。当更强大或更危险的新模型部署时，开发者应创建风险报告并论证模型部署的合理性。

Abstract: Frontier AI companies first deploy their most advanced models internally, for weeks or months of safety testing, evaluation, and iteration, before a possible public release. For example, Anthropic recently developed a new class of model with advanced cyberoffense-relevant capabilities, Mythos Preview, which was available internally for at least six weeks before it was publicly announced. This internal use creates risks that external deployment frameworks may fail to address.
  Legal frameworks, notably California's Transparency in Frontier Artificial Intelligence Act (SB 53), New York's Responsible AI Safety And Education (RAISE) Act, and the EU's General-Purpose AI Code of Practice, all discuss risks from internal AI use. They require frontier developers to make and implement plans for how to manage risks from internal use, and to produce internal use risk reports describing their safeguards and any residual risks. This guide provides a harmonized standard for companies to produce internal use risk reports suitable for all three regulatory frameworks. It is addressed primarily to evaluation and safety teams at frontier AI developers, and secondarily to regulators and auditors seeking to understand what good reporting looks like.
  Given the pace of AI R&D automation and the limited external visibility into how companies use their most capable models internally, regular and detailed risk reporting may be one of the few mechanisms available to ensure that the risks from internal AI use are identified and managed before they materialize. Whenever a substantially more capable or riskier model is deployed internally, the developer should create a risk report and argue why the model is safe to deploy. We structure the reporting framework around two threat vectors -- autonomous AI misbehavior and insider threats -- and three risk factors for each: means, motive, and opportunity.

</details>


### [85] [Coasting Through Class: Learning Opportunity Loss from Practice Avoidance During Individual Seatwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25014)
*Ashish Gurung,Jordan Gutterman,Danielle R. Thomas,Mingyu Feng,Vincent Aleven,Kenneth R. Koedinger*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究定义了"coasting behavior"（滑行行为）来量化学生在数学练习中浪费的机会时间。通过ASSISTments平台日志（N=1,425），发现学生仅将40%的可用课堂时间用于练习，其余60%为"滑行时间"（包括延迟开始36%、中途闲置2%、提前停止62%）。滑行行为表现出中等时间稳定性（G=0.73-0.71），与性别和IEP状态显著相关。完成作业后继续练习的"额外努力"与更好的标准化测试成绩相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脱离行为测量主要关注任务内行为（如游戏系统、走神），而任务回避导致的练习时间损失研究不足。本研究旨在扩展测量框架，纳入会话级别的脱离指标，填补机会时间损失研究的空白。

Method: 使用ASSISTments平台日志数据（N=1,425），将传统任务内指标（闲置时间）与两个新的会话级指标（延迟开始、早期停止）结合，定义"coasted time"为综合时间损失指标。通过G系数评估时间稳定性，分析人口统计学差异，并比较"额外努力"学生的标准化测试表现。

Result: 学生仅使用40%可用课堂时间进行数学练习；60%为滑行时间（延迟开始36%、中途闲置2%、早期停止62%）。滑行行为具有中等时间稳定性（G=0.73-0.71）。排除完成作业导致的早期停止后，滑行时间仍占32%。滑行行为在性别和IEP状态上存在显著差异。完成首项作业后继续练习的学生标准化测试成绩显著更高。

Conclusion: "滑行行为"为研究机会损失提供了新视角，整合了会话级与任务内脱离测量。平台设计应支持持续参与，提高练习时间的生产性利用。学生完成作业后的坚持行为与学业成就正相关，具有重要的教育干预价值。

Abstract: Measures of disengagement provide insights into unproductive use of learning opportunities. Although measures of active disengagement, such as gaming the system and mind-wandering, are well studied, loss of practice time due to outright task avoidance remains relatively understudied. The current study addresses this gap by extending existing within-task measures (idle time) with two new session-level measures (delayed start and early stop) to capture loss of practice time due to task avoidance. We characterize the combined lost time as coasted time and the associated behavior as coasting behavior. Using ASSISTments logs (N = 1,425), we find that students dedicate only 40% of available classwork time to math practice and coast through the remaining 60%. Of the coasted time, 36% resulted from delayed starts, 2% from mid-practice idling, and 62% from stopping early. Delayed start and early stop showed moderate temporal stability (G = 0.73 and 0.71, respectively), suggesting that coasting is a consistent behavioral pattern. Even after excluding early stops attributable to assignment completion (i.e., early stop = 0), coasted time remained substantial at 32%. While we observe significant differences in coasting by gender and IEP status, we do not observe them by other demographic factors or school locale. Critically, students who continued working beyond the first assignment completion ("extra effort") performed significantly better on standardized tests. For research, coasting offers a new lens on opportunity loss by combining session-level disengagement with within-task disengagement. For practitioners, our results highlight the need for platform affordances that support sustained engagement and more productive use of available practice time.

</details>


### [86] [Adoption of TikTok as a Learning Tool in Physical Education: Evidence from the Philippines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25049)
*Vanessa B. Sibug,Jan Henry B. Sunga,Emerson Q. Fernando,Roe Vincent S. Ovejas,Arjan Gil S. Mendoza,Trisha Anne A. Onofre,Agnes R. Regala,John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究以菲律宾1,075名大学生为样本，探讨TikTok作为体育教育学习工具的采纳因素。研究结合技术接受模型和使用与满足理论，采用结构方程模型验证发现，感知有用性和感知易用性是影响使用意愿的最强预测因子，表明TikTok作为有趣且易获取的学习媒介，能有效支持体育教育的主动学习和参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着短视频平台在教育领域的应用日益普及，了解学生采纳TikTok进行体育教育学习的驱动因素具有重要意义。该研究旨在填补菲律宾情境下关于短格式视频平台在体育教育中应用研究的空白。

Method: 采用横断面研究设计，运用结构方程模型(SEM)进行分析。基于技术接受模型(TAM)和使用与满足理论(UGT)构建理论框架，检验信息寻求、个人认同、社交互动、娱乐性、感知有用性(PU)、感知易用性(PEOU)与使用意愿(IU)之间的关系。

Result: 感知有用性(PU)和感知易用性(PEOU)是预测TikTok体育教育内容使用意愿的最强因素。样本包括1,075名定期使用TikTok的用户，平均年龄19岁，多数为女性。

Conclusion: 研究表明TikTok作为PE学习工具具有显著潜力，尤其是其有用性和易用性是影响使用意愿的关键因素。研究为菲律宾情境下短视频平台在体育教育中的应用提供了实证支持，丰富了该领域的学术讨论。

Abstract: This study examines the factors that influence the adoption of TikTok as a learning tool for physical education (PE)-related content among tertiary students in the Philippines. The study applies the Technology Acceptance Model (TAM) and Uses and Gratification Theory (UGT) to assess Information Seeking, Personal Identity, Social Interaction, Entertainment, Perceived Usefulness (PU), Perceived Ease of Use (PEOU), and Intention to Use (IU). A cross-sectional design and Structural Equation Modeling (SEM) were employed. The sample included 1,075 regular TikTok users with an average age of 19 years, the majority of whom were female. The analysis revealed that PU and PEOU were the strongest predictors of IU TikTok for PE related content. The results indicate that TikTok provides an engaging and accessible medium that supports active learning and participation in PE. The study offers empirical evidence from the Philippines and contributes to the academic discussion on the role of short-form video platforms in PE.

</details>


### [87] [Does This Even Matter in the Real World? Real World Problems in Foundational Theory Courses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25082)
*Anna Kuznetsova*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 一项关于在离散数学和概率论课程中引入基于现实世界应用的家庭作业问题的教学研究。研究发现，绝大多数学生（约93%）在课程开始和结束时都认为课程内容与自身相关，学生对这类问题持积极态度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决计算机科学基础课程教学中常见的学生焦虑问题——认为离散数学和概率论过于抽象、与未来职业无关。研究者试图通过引入真实应用场景来提高学生对课程相关性的认知。

Method: 在入门理论课程中引入基于现实应用的作业题（包括法庭案例、代码正确性和机器学习伦理），并在对学期初和学期末对学生进行态度调查，比较学生对课程内容相关性的看法变化。

Result: 出乎意料的是，不到7%的学生在学期初认为材料与他们无关，学期末这一比例同样很低。调查和定性反馈表明，学生喜欢这些问题，并希望在未来课程中继续提供。

Conclusion: 基于现实应用的作业问题受到学生欢迎，可能有助于提高学生参与度；大多数学生从一开始就认为离散数学和概率论是相关的，这挑战了教师对此类课程学生态度的假设。

Abstract: Discrete mathematics and probability theory contain foundational material for computer scientists. Despite their importance, instructors often worry that students will find these courses to be too abstract and seemingly disconnected from their future careers. For this research project, we introduced homework questions throughout our introductory theory courses based on real world applications of the course content. Areas of application included a court case, code correctness, and machine learning ethics. We surveyed students at the beginning and end of the term on their attitudes toward the relevance of the course material. Our results, surprisingly, indicate that a small minority of students (less than 7%) expected the material to be irrelevant to them at the start of the term, and a similarly small number believed that at the end of the term. Our surveys and qualitative feedback also indicate students enjoyed having the problems and wanted them to continue being offered in future iterations of the courses.

</details>


### [88] [A Faceted Proposal for Transparent Attribution of AI-Assisted Text Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25346)
*Geraldo Xexéo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文针对当前AI辅助写作披露不充分的问题，提出一个从段落到文档多粒度、多层次的分面模型，用于详细记录AI在文本创作中的介入方式、审查过程和责任归属，旨在建立AI写作披露的标准化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI使用披露仅说明"是否"使用AI，缺乏对"如何使用、在何处介入、如何审查"等关键信息的透明说明，无法满足对创作责任和智力贡献的清晰界定需求。

Method: 设计了一个分层的多面模型，从文档、章节、段落四个粒度级别描述AI辅助文本生产过程；核心模型包含形式（Form）、生成（Generation）、评估（Evaluation）三个维度；扩展模型增加意图（Intent）、控制（Control）、可追溯性（Traceability）三个维度。

Result: 提供了一个基于本文自身生产过程的实例验证，展示了模型在实践中的可应用性和可操作性。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可扩展的分层模型，从核心三要素（形式、生成、评估）扩展至完整六要素（意图、控制、可追溯性），为AI写作披露提供了可操作的基线标准。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly integrated into writing processes, challenging traditional notions of authorship, responsibility, and intellectual contribution. Current disclosure practices usually indicate whether AI was used, but rarely explain how it was used, where it intervened, or how its output was reviewed. This paper proposes a faceted model for representing AI-assisted text production at the levels of documents, chapters, sections, and paragraphs. The proposal introduces a core model based on Form, Generation, and Evaluation, and an extended model that adds Intent, Control, and Traceability. The model is positioned as a minimal operational baseline with extensibility toward higher-fidelity representations. A worked example based on the production of this article demonstrates applicability.

</details>


### [89] [Large language models eroding science understanding: an experimental study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25639)
*Harry Collins,Hartmut Grote,Paul Newbury,Patrick Sutton,Simon Thorne*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Study demonstrates that LLMs are highly susceptible to manipulation through fringe scientific material, producing convincing yet misleading information that contradicts expert consensus and is hard for non-experts to detect.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The research investigates whether large language models can reliably answer scientific questions, particularly given the ease with which they can be influenced by unreliable fringe scientific material, raising concerns about their deployment in scientific communication contexts.

Method: The authors modified custom LLMs to prioritize knowledge from fringe scientific papers on the Fine Structure Constant and Gravitational Waves, then compared responses with those from domain experts and standard (unmodified) LLMs through controlled experiments.

Result: The manipulated models generated fluent, convincing answers that contradicted scientific consensus, yet were difficult for non-experts to identify as misleading—demonstrating that LLM outputs can be systematically biased without obvious signs of low quality.

Conclusion: LLMs are vulnerable to manipulation and cannot reliably replace expert judgment, posing significant risks for public understanding of science and potential misinformation spread.

Abstract: This paper is under review in AI and Ethics This study examines whether large language models (LLMs) can reliably answer scientific questions and demonstrates how easily they can be influenced by fringe scientific material. The authors modified custom LLMs to prioritise knowledge in selected fringe papers on the Fine Structure Constant and Gravitational Waves, then compared their responses with those of domain experts and standard LLMs. The altered models produced fluent, convincing answers that contradicted scientific consensus and were difficult for non-experts to detect as misleading. The results show that LLMs are vulnerable to manipulation and cannot replace expert judgment, highlighting risks for public understanding of science and the potential spread of misinformation.

</details>


### [90] [Curiosity and Metacognition: Towards a Unified Framework for Learning and Education in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25648)
*Chloé Desvaux,Rania Abdelghani,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer,Hélène Sauzéon*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了好奇心与元认知作为自主学习和自我调节学习的关键驱动因素的关联，并提出整合行为、计算和心理教育维度的统一框架。研究表明，课堂中的好奇心干预效果参差不齐，对学习困难者效果更显著，提示需要个性化方法。此外，生成式AI虽能为个性化探究提供前所未有的可扩展性，但其默认交互模式可能损害好奇心驱动学习的动态机制，因此需要策略将AI从认知捷径转化为支持认知发展的伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究对好奇心和元认知的整合理解不足，且生成式AI对教育实践的影响尚未被充分探讨。本文旨在弥合这一理论与实践的鸿沟，为自主学习和AI辅助教育提供概念框架。

Method: 综合近期研究成果，提出整合行为、计算和心理教育维度的统一框架；从教育视角评估课堂好奇心增强干预措施的有效性；分析LLM默认交互模式对好奇心驱动学习的潜在风险。

Result: 研究发现：(1)好奇心本质上依赖于元认知监控与控制过程；(2)现有课堂干预效果不一，对学习困难学生效果更明显，需个性化方案；(3)LLM的默认交互模式可能成为认知捷径，阻碍好奇心驱动学习的动态发展。

Conclusion: 构建好奇心-元认知整合框架对于理解自主学习至关重要；教育干预必须考虑个体差异；将AI转化为认知发展伙伴而非替代思考的工具，需要重新设计AI交互策略，以维持而非削弱学习者的好奇心和元认知投入。

Abstract: This chapter examines the relationship between curiosity and metacognition as critical drivers of autonomous and self-regulated learning. We synthesize recent research to propose a unified framework integrating behavioral, computational, and psychoeducational dimensions, arguing that curiosity, i.e. the intrinsic drive to acquire new knowledge, relies fundamentally on metacognitive monitoring and control. From an educational perspective, we evaluate interventions designed to enhance curiosity in classroom settings. While promising, our review indicates that these interventions yield mixed results, often proving differentially effective for struggling learners, thereby underscoring the necessity for approaches tailored to individual profiles. Finally, we address the paradigm shift introduced by Generative AI. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer unprecedented scalability for personalized inquiry, we argue that their default interaction modes pose significant risks to the dynamics of curiosity-driven learning. To mitigate these challenges, we review strategies to transform AI from a potential cognitive shortcut into a powerful partner for sustained epistemic development.

</details>


### [91] [Hands-on PDC in Undergraduate Computing Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25812)
*Hala ElAarag,Anas Gamal Aly*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper presents a practical assignment where undergraduates use the HiPerGator supercomputer to implement and benchmark matrix multiplication (Python/C with POSIX threads and OpenMP), providing experiential learning in HPC environments that are typically inaccessible at the undergraduate level.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDC is conceptually challenging in undergraduate curriculum; students lack hands-on experience with real HPC environments despite learning theoretical concepts.

Method: Students implemented and benchmarked matrix multiplication using Python and C (POSIX threads, OpenMP) on HiPerGator supercomputer, navigating batch scheduling, core allocation, and performance tuning.

Result: Three-year evaluation across multiple course offerings demonstrates improved student comprehension of parallelism and multithreading through project-based learning on authentic HPC infrastructure.

Conclusion: Structured access to real HPC infrastructure deepens student understanding of parallelism and multithreading concepts.

Abstract: Parallel and Distributed Computing (PDC) is a critical yet conceptually challenging area of the undergraduate computer science curriculum. While students often encounter these concepts in theory, few gain exposure to experience in real high-performance computing (HPC) environments. Research shows that when students are engaged in project-based learning they retain knowledge more effectively. They also develop a deeper understanding of concepts taught in the classroom. This paper presents a practical assignment in which students engage directly with the University of Florida's HiPerGator supercomputer to implement and benchmark matrix multiplication using Python and C (via POSIX threads and OpenMP). Students navigate batch scheduling, core allocation, and performance tuning, experiences that are rarely accessible at the undergraduate level. We describe the assignment in detail and provide a three-year evaluation across multiple course offerings, highlighting how structured access to real HPC infrastructure can deepen student understanding of parallelism and multithreading.

</details>


### [92] [Three Models of RLHF Annotation: Extension, Evidence, and Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25895)
*Steve Coyne*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文探讨了RLHF等偏好对齐方法中人类标注判断的规范性角色，提出三种概念模型：扩展（标注者延伸设计者判断）、证据（标注者提供独立事实证据）、权威（标注者作为更广泛群体的代表拥有决定权）。作者通过综述相关里程碑论文，揭示了这些模型如何被隐含使用，描述了混用这些模型导致的失效模式，并建议根据维度特性选择最合适的管道模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前偏好对齐方法（尤其是RLHF）依赖人类标注者的判断，但这些判断的规范性角色很少被明确化，这导致实践中存在概念混淆和潜在的失效模式。

Method: 作者区分了三种概念模型（扩展、证据、权威），并通过综述RLHF及相关方法的里程碑论文，来说明这些模型如何被隐含使用。

Result: 作者识别了三模型各自的失效模式，提供了在它们之间进行选择的规范性标准。

Conclusion: 作者的核心建议是将注释分解为可分离的维度，并为每个维度量身定制最适合的模型管道，而不是寻求单一的统一管道。

Abstract: Preference-based alignment methods, most prominently Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF), use the judgments of human annotators to shape large language model behaviour. However, the normative role of these judgments is rarely made explicit. I distinguish three conceptual models of that role. The first is extension: annotators extend the system designers' own judgments about what outputs should be. The second is evidence: annotators provide independent evidence about some facts, whether moral, social or otherwise. The third is authority: annotators have some independent authority (as representatives of the broader population) to determine system outputs. I argue that these models have implications for how RLHF pipelines should solicit, validate and aggregate annotations. I survey landmark papers in the literature on RLHF and related methods to illustrate how they implicitly draw on these models, describe failure modes that come from unintentionally or intentionally conflating them, and offer normative criteria for choosing among them. My central recommendation is that RLHF pipeline designers should decompose annotation into separable dimensions and tailor each pipeline to the model most appropriate for that dimension, rather than seeking a single unified pipeline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Co-Director: Agentic Generative Video Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24842)
*Yale Song,Yiwen Song,Nick Losier,Nathan Hodson,Ye Jin,Rhyard Zhu,Yan Xu,Daniel Vlasic,Carina Claassen,Jasmine Leon,Khanh G. LeViet,Zack Chomyn,Joe Timmons,Brett Slatkin,Scott Penberthy,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI
