To this end, we introduce RecursiveMAS, a recursive multi-agent framework that casts the entire system as a unified latent-space recursive computation. RecursiveMAS connects heterogeneous agents as a collaboration loop through the lightweight RecursiveLink module, enabling in-distribution latent thoughts generation and cross-agent latent state transfer. To optimize our framework, we develop an inner-outer loop learning algorithm for iterative whole-system co-optimization through shared gradient-based credit assignment across recursion rounds. Theoretical analyses of runtime complexity and learning dynamics establish that RecursiveMAS is more efficient than standard text-based MAS and maintains stable gradients during recursive training. Empirically, we instantiate RecursiveMAS under 4 representative agent collaboration patterns and evaluate across 9 benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, medicine, search, and code generation. In comparison with advanced single/multi-agent and recursive computation baselines, RecursiveMAS consistently delivers an average accuracy improvement of 8.3%, together with 1.2$\times$-2.4$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup, and 34.6%-75.6% token usage reduction. Code and Data are provided in https://recursivemas.github.io.

</details>


### [128] [A Quantitative Definition of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10873)
*Kang-Sin Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种基底无关的智能量化定义：智能密度为独立输出对数与描述长度之比，并区分"记忆"（描述长度随输出增长）与"知道"（描述长度固定但输出无限）。通过有限机制产生无限正确输出的能力被定义为"知道"，并最终反驳了Searle的"句法不足以产生语义"论点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能定义缺乏操作性和定量性，且无法在基底无关的连续统上比较不同系统（如逻辑门到大脑）。此外，Searle的中文房间论证认为句法不足以产生语义，需要从形式化角度予以反驳。

Method: 提出三个核心量化指标：（1）智能密度 = 独立输出对数 / 总描述长度；（2）知道 vs 记忆的判据；（3）上下文性度量 = 条件Kolmogorov复杂度的倒数。

Result: 智能可被量化为在一组可判定正确性的输出中，通过有限机制产生无限正确输出的能力。通过上下文性度量统一了正确性和独立性，并证明对任何可判定正确性的领域，句法足以支撑语义。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一套完整的智能形式化框架，通过定义"知道"（knowing）作为智能的核心判据，并最终论证了形式化定义足以支撑语义（反驳Searle的第三个前提），同时强调意义（meaning）是选择和排序产生正确输出的函数。

Abstract: We propose an operational, quantitative definition of intelligence for arbitrary physical systems. The intelligence density of a system is the ratio of the logarithm of its independent outputs to its total description length. A system memorizes if its description length grows with its output count; it knows if its description length remains fixed while its output count diverges. The criterion for knowing is generalization. A system knows its domain if a single finite mechanism can produce correct outputs across an unbounded range of inputs, rather than storing each answer individually. The definition places intelligence on a substrate-independent continuum from logic gates to brains. We then argue that meaning over a domain is a selection and ordering of functions that produces correct outputs where correctness is specifiable. We also define a measure of contextuality of an output as the inverse of its conditional Kolmogorov complexity given the context of prior outputs, which unifies correctness and independence into a single condition. Together, these refute Searle's third premise, that syntax is insufficient for semantics, over any domain where correctness is specifiable.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Spark Policy Toolkit: Semantic Contracts and Scalable Execution for Policy Learning in Spark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25061)
*Zeyu Bai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Spark Policy Toolkit 解决了 Spark 环境下大规模策略学习的两大瓶颈：基于行的 Python 执行导致的推理效率低下，以及驱动程序端候选物化导致的分裂搜索脆弱性。该工具箱通过 mapInPandas/mapInArrow 实现分区级向量化推理，以及去收集的分裂搜索来分布式评分候选特征。在 40 节点 Databricks 集群上，mapInArrow 达到 472 万行/秒（1000万行）和 723 万行/秒（5000万行）的吞吐量，且在 F=10 到 F=1000 的特征规模下保持语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Spark 策略学习流程存在两个耦合的系统级问题：一是行级 Python 执行使得大规模推理不切实际，二是驱动程序端的候选物化使得分裂搜索在特征规模扩展时变得脆弱，无法保证策略语义在分布式环境下的正确性。

Method: 提出 Spark Policy Toolkit，引入两个 Spark 原生原语：（1）基于 mapInPandas 和 mapInArrow 的分区初始化向量化推理；（2）去收集的分裂搜索，在执行器端分布式评分候选特征。两个原语均遵循固定输入语义契约：相同的行顺序、特征顺序、处理方式、分裂边界必须保留每行分数向量、最优分裂决策和端到端学习策略输出。

Result: 在 40 节点 Databricks 集群上，mapInArrow 在 1000 万匹配行下达 472 万行/秒，5000 万行下达 723 万行/秒。去收集分裂搜索在 F=10 至 F=1000 范围内（124,000 候选行）保持有效。24 种后端消融设置中 mapInArrow 赢得 18 次，mapInPandas 赢得 6 次。enforce fixed-input lock 后，所有 6 种重分区/合并/混排扰动均保留相同签名；未锁定前均漂移。

Conclusion: 核心贡献不仅是速度：吞吐量和去收集执行是让策略语义在 Spark 规模下得以存活的关键机制。后端选择应视为工作负载依赖而非通用最优解。

Abstract: Custom policy-learning pipelines in Spark fail for two coupled systems reasons: rowwise Python execution makes inference impractical, and driver-side candidate materialization makes split search fragile at feature scale. We present Spark Policy Toolkit, a semantics-governed systems toolkit for scalable policy learning in Spark. The toolkit provides two Spark-native primitives: partition-initialized vectorized inference through mapInPandas and mapInArrow, and collect-less split search that scores candidates on executors. Both primitives are governed by one fixed-input semantic contract: the same rows, feature order, treatment vocabulary, preprocessing manifest, and split boundaries must preserve per-row score vectors, best-split decisions, and end-to-end learned policy outputs. The evaluation combines practical baseline ladders, backend parity checks, measured split-search scale results, synthetic and Hillstrom end-to-end policy preservation, missingness stress, partition and order perturbation tests, quantile-boundary sensitivity, and a concrete adversarial failure catalog. On a 40-worker Databricks cluster, mapInArrow reaches 4.72M rows/s at 10M matched rows and 7.23M rows/s at 50M rows, while collect-less split search remains valid from F = 10 through F = 1000 with 124000 candidate rows, where the driver-collect baseline is intentionally skipped. Across 24 backend-ablation settings, mapInArrow wins 18 while mapInPandas wins 6, so the paper treats backend choice as workload-dependent rather than universal. Once the fixed-input lock is enforced, all six tested repartition/coalesce/shuffle perturbations preserve identical signatures; before lock, all six drift. The central result is not speed alone: throughput and collect-less execution are the mechanisms that let policy semantics survive at Spark scale.

</details>


### [130] [CacheFlow: Efficient LLM Serving with 3D-Parallel KV Cache Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25080)
*Sean Nian,Jiahao Fang,Qilong Feng,Zhiyu Wu,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CacheFlow通过将KV缓存恢复重新定义为多维并行执行问题，在长上下文LLM服务场景下实现了显著的性能提升，相比现有方法将首token生成时间(TTFT)降低10%-62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存恢复已成为长上下文LLM工作负载（包括多轮对话、检索增强生成和智能体流程）服务的主要瓶颈。现有方法将恢复视为重计算与I/O传输之间的权衡，但未能充分利用token间、层间和分布式部署的并行性，且忽视了批处理服务下的资源竞争问题。

Method: CacheFlow提出三个核心创新：(1)统一的3D并行抽象，跨越token、层和GPU维度；(2)沿transformer推理的结构依赖关系实现重计算与I/O的细粒度重叠；(3)批量感知的双指针调度器，通过优先处理边际重计算成本降低最高的操作来联合优化计算和I/O分配。

Result: 评估结果表明，CacheFlow在多种模型、工作负载和硬件配置下，相比现有先进方法将Time-To-First-Token (TTFT)降低10%-62%。

Conclusion: 该工作将KV缓存恢复问题从传统的权衡优化视角拓展为多维并行执行问题，为长上下文LLM服务系统提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: KV cache restoration has emerged as a dominant bottleneck in serving long-context LLM workloads, including multi-turn conversations, retrieval-augmented generation, and agentic pipelines. Existing approaches treat restoration as a per-request tradeoff between recomputation and I/O transfer, recomputing KV states from scratch or offloading them from external storage (e.g., CPU memory or remote machines). However, existing advances fail to exploit parallelism across tokens, layers, and distributed deployments, and critically ignore resource contention under batched serving. We present CacheFlow, a KV cache restoration framework that rethinks cache restoration as a multi-dimensional parallel execution problem. CacheFlow introduces a unified 3D parallelism abstraction across tokens, layers, and GPUs, enabling fine-grained overlap of recomputation and I/O along the structural dependencies of transformer inference. At the core of CacheFlow is a batch-aware two-pointer scheduler that jointly optimizes compute and I/O allocation across requests by prioritizing operations with the highest marginal reduction in recomputation cost. Our evaluations show that CacheFlow reduces Time-To-First-Token (TTFT) by 10%-62% over existing advances across diverse models, workloads, and hardware.

</details>


### [131] [CUDA Kernel Optimization and Counter-Free Performance Analysis for Depthwise Convolution in Cloud Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25422)
*Huriyeh Babak,Melanie Schaller*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对S4ConvD中使用的depthwise convolution进行了系统性的CUDA kernel优化研究，并提出了无需硬件性能计数器的云环境兼容性能分析方法。通过比较naive、global-memory-coalesced、shared-memory cache-blocked和warp-tiled四种kernel变体，warp-tiled方案实现了3.26×的卷积运行时间缩减和1.29×的端到端训练加速，其中weight-gradient路径是主要性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度可分离卷积的高效GPU执行不仅取决于算术吞吐量，更受内存访问效率、片上数据复用和执行映射的制约。当前云环境对硬件性能计数器访问受限，传统的profiling分析方法难以适用，需要一种可复现的、无需特权访问的性能分析方法来指导kernel优化决策。

Method: 采用控制变量法，固定operator、model、dataset和训练配置，仅变化CUDA kernel实现。评估了naive、global-memory-coalesced、shared-memory cache-blocked和warp-tiled四种kernel变体，覆盖forward、input-gradient和weight-gradient三条执行路径。性能分析采用counter-free方法论，结合CUDA event计时、执行路径分解、解析内存流量建模、有效带宽估计和roofline分析。

Result: Warp-tiled kernel相比naive基线减少卷积运行时间3.26×，端到端训练加速达1.29×。Forward和input-gradient路径从局部性优化和片上数据复用中获益显著，而reduction主导的weight-gradient路径仍为主要性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 在受限的云环境中，即使无法访问硬件性能计数器，仍可实现有意义的架构级GPU kernel分析。通过系统性的kernel优化，特别是warp-tiling技术，可显著提升深度可分离卷积的训练效率。该counter-free分析框架具有良好的可复现性，适用于云端GPU性能评估。

Abstract: Efficient GPU execution of convolution operators is governed by memory-access efficiency, on-chip data reuse, and execution mapping rather than arithmetic throughput alone. This paper presents a controlled operator-level study of CUDA kernel optimization for the depthwise convolution used in Structured State Space Model Convolutional Diagonal (S4ConvD), together with a cloud-compatible, counter-free performance analysis methodology. The operator, model, dataset, and training configuration are fixed, and only the CUDA kernel implementation is varied. The evaluated CUDA kernels comprise naive, global-memory-coalesced, shared-memory cache-blocked, and warp-tiled variants, covering forward, input-gradient, and weight-gradient execution paths under steady-state training conditions. Performance is characterized using a counter-free methodology that combines CUDA-event timing, execution-path decomposition, analytically derived memory-traffic modeling, effective-bandwidth estimation, and roofline analysis. This enables profiling-like architectural insights without requiring hardware performance counters or privileged profiling access. The warp-tiled kernel reduces convolution runtime by $3.26\times$ relative to the naive CUDA baseline, while end-to-end training speedup reaches $1.29\times$. A PyTorch implementation is used separately for numerical validation and runtime context, but is not treated as a controlled architectural baseline. Forward and input-gradient paths benefit substantially from improved locality and on-chip data reuse, whereas the reduction-dominated weight-gradient path remains the primary bottleneck. The results demonstrate that meaningful architecture-level GPU kernel analysis can be performed reproducibly in restricted cloud environments, even without access to hardware performance counters.

</details>


### [132] [Economical and ecological impact of sector coupling applied to computing clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25540)
*P. Bechtle,O. Freyermuth,M. Geffers,M. Giffels,M. Hübner,F. Kirfel,J. Kreutz,S. Krieg,S. Matberg,M. Schnepf*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究利用德国电力生产数据，模拟高性能计算集群的动态运营策略，根据可再生能源充足时的电力消耗进行调度，以降低碳排放和运营成本，同时考虑硬件采购成本和嵌入式排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可再生能源比例增加，电力生产波动性增大。高性能计算集群对结果时间延迟容忍度较高，成为测试动态运营策略的理想场景，可实现电力系统剩余负荷的优化调度，降低环境影响和成本。

Method: 使用德国公开电力生产数据模拟简化集群运营；分别计算最优利用率及相关碳排放和成本降低；考虑硬件采购成本和嵌入式排放；在两个验证期内评估固定计算目标的稳定性；进行参数修改模拟以评估未来应用潜力。

Result: 确定了最优利用率及相应碳排放和成本降低；验证了固定计算目标的稳定性。

Conclusion: 动态运营策略可实现显著的环境和经济效益；模拟结果验证了未来进一步应用的潜力。

Abstract: The rising share of abundant renewable energy inevitably increases volatility in the electricity production. The concept of sector coupling means that the volatility of electricity production to a large degree can be absorbed by dispatching electricity consumption whenever excess renewable energy is available. A system that is dynamically operated based on this principle can lower its total environmental impact. In addition, operational costs might be reducible as electricity prizes strongly depend on the residual load of the energy system. High-performance computing clusters in the field of science represent an ideal testing ground for such dynamic operation. Short-term delays in computing results due to electricity production being associated with high costs or carbon emissions are often negligible, provided that an overall computing target remains constant over long time periods. This study simulates the simplified operation of computing clusters using publicly available data on electricity production in Germany. The optimal utilisation along with associated carbon emission and cost reductions are determined separately. Hardware acquisition costs and embedded emissions are taken into account. The stability of a fixed computing target given the determined utilisation optima is evaluated in two validation periods. Additional simulations with modified parameters are carried out to estimate potential conditions under which dynamic operation of a computing cluster would continue to enable savings in the future.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [SWE-QA: A Dataset and Benchmark for Complex Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24814)
*Laïla Elkoussy,Julien Perez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-QA是一个用于评估多跳代码理解能力的基准数据集，包含9,072道选择题，来源于SWE-bench的12个Python代码库。主要评估Declaration-and-Call和Interacting-Entity两类推理模式。实验表明最佳模型准确率为74.41%，密集架构比MoE模型表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码理解基准测试仅关注孤立的代码片段，而实际开发中程序员需要连接多个分散代码段的信息。该研究旨在弥合简化评估任务与真实软件开发所需复杂推理之间的差距。

Method: 采用基于解析的实体提取方法，结合大语言模型辅助题目构建，使用经过验证的干扰项来区分真正理解与表面模式匹配。评估了15个语言模型（参数规模从360M到671B）。

Result: 最佳性能达到74.41%准确率。密集架构在多跳推理任务中一致性地比混合专家模型高出10-14个百分点。推理增强型变体模型的表现增益不一致。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型在多跳代码推理方面仍面临重大挑战，模型架构类型（密集vs MoE）对性能影响显著，简单的参数规模扩大和推理增强策略未能带来稳定提升。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce SWE-QA, a text and code corpus aimed at benchmarking multi-hop code comprehension, addressing the gap between simplified evaluation tasks and the complex reasoning required in real-world software development. While existing code understanding benchmarks focus on isolated snippets, developers must routinely connect information across multiple dispersed code segments. The dataset comprises 9,072 multiple-choice questions systematically generated from 12 Python repositories of SWE-bench, evaluating several recurrent reasoning patterns like Declaration-and-Call questions that link entity definitions to their usage, and Interacting-Entity questions that examine the dynamic relationships among multiple collaborating components. Generated through parsing-based entity extraction and Large Language Model assisted question construction with carefully validated distractors, the benchmark distinguishes genuine comprehension from superficial pattern matching. Evaluation of 15 language models (360M to 671B parameters) reveals significant challenges in multi-hop reasoning, with best performance reaching 74.41% accuracy. Dense architectures consistently outperform mixture-of-experts models by 10-14 percentage points, while reasoning-enhanced variants show inconsistent benefits.

</details>


### [134] [Programming with Data: Test-Driven Data Engineering for Self-Improving LLMs from Raw Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24819)
*Chenkai Pan,Xinglong Xu,Yuhang Xu,Yujun Wu,Siyuan Li,Jintao Chen,Conghui He,Jingxuan Wei,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据编程"（Programming with Data）框架，将训练数据开发与软件开发类比，使模型失败可追溯至特定数据缺陷并通过针对性补丁修复，为语言模型的人类专业知识可靠工程化建立原则基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从文本向大语言模型可靠迁移专业人类知识的根本挑战。现有微调方法缺乏反馈机制：当模型在领域任务中失败时，无法诊断训练数据的缺陷，只能盲目追加数据。

Method: 采用结构化知识表示作为训练数据与评估的共同基础，建立类比映射：训练数据→源代码（指定模型应学内容），模型训练→编译，基准测试→单元测试，失败驱动的数据修复→调试。在此框架下，模型失败被分解为概念级差距和推理链断裂，可追溯至特定数据缺陷并通过针对性补丁修复。

Result: 在16个横跨自然科学、工程、生物医学和社会科学的学科中验证了该方法。修复周期可在不同模型规模和架构中产生一致改进，且不损害通用能力。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了"数据编程"的原理框架，通过将训练数据与模型行为之间的映射关系形式化，实现了从文本到语言模型的专业知识可靠迁移。通过16个学科的验证，该方法可跨模型规模和架构产生一致的改进，且不损害通用能力。研究成果以知识库、基准测试套件和训练语料库的形式开放共享。

Abstract: Reliably transferring specialized human knowledge from text into large language models remains a fundamental challenge in artificial intelligence. Fine-tuning on domain corpora has enabled substantial capability gains, but the process operates without feedback: when a model fails on a domain task, there is no method to diagnose what is deficient in the training data, and the only recourse is to add more data indiscriminately. Here we show that when a structured knowledge representation extracted from the source corpus serves as the shared foundation for both training data and evaluation, the complete data-engineering lifecycle maps onto the software development lifecycle in a precise and operative way: training data becomes source code specifying what the model should learn, model training becomes compilation, benchmarking becomes unit testing, and failure-driven data repair becomes debugging. Under this correspondence, model failures decompose into concept-level gaps and reasoning-chain breaks that can be traced back to specific deficiencies in the data and repaired through targeted patches, with each repair cycle producing consistent improvements across model scales and architectures without degrading general capabilities. We formalize this principle as Programming with Data and instantiate it across sixteen disciplines spanning the natural sciences, engineering, biomedicine, and the social sciences, releasing a structured knowledge base, benchmark suite, and training corpus as open resources. By demonstrating that the relationship between training data and model behaviour is structurally traceable and systematically repairable, this work establishes a principled foundation for the reliable engineering of human expertise into language models.

</details>


### [135] [FGDM: Reasoning Aware Multi-Agentic Framework for Software Bug Detection using Chain of Thought and Tree of Thought Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24831)
*Srita Padmanabhuni,Bhargavi Karuturi,Jerusha Karen Indupalli,Santhan Reddy Chilla,Vivek Yelleti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出FGDM框架，利用大语言模型和多智能体系统进行代码bug检测与修复。该框架通过将代码转换为流程图、使用思维链和思维树提示，结合FAISS向量数据库检索历史bug修复案例，在100个程序上验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在自动化软件bug检测中缺乏对代码的全局理解能力，尤其在处理大型互联代码库或复杂模块化程序时性能下降明显。而大语言模型已被证明能有效捕获代码库中多个互联模块之间的依赖关系。

Method: 提出流程图驱动的多智能体框架(FGDM)，包含四个顺序执行的智能体：代码转流程图、识别错误代码段、生成修复代码。框架采用思维链(COT)和思维树(TOT)提示技术，并集成FAISS向量数据库检索相似历史bug及修复方案。

Result: 在Ansible、Black、FastAPI、Keras、Luigi、Matplotlib、Pandas、Scrapy、SpaCy、Tornado等10个项目共100个C和Python程序上进行实验。FGDM在Python和C程序上的表现均优于现有方法：Levenshtein距离分别降低24.33和8.37，余弦相似度分别达到0.951和0.974。

Conclusion: FGDM框架通过流程图表示、多智能体协作及思维提示技术，有效提升了代码bug检测与修复的性能，在多种编程语言和项目类型上展现了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Deep Learning methods are becoming prominent in automated software bug detection; however, they lack the global understanding of the given code. Consequently, their performance tends to degrade, especially when they are applied to large interconnected code bases or complex modular programs. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have proven to be effective at capturing dependencies among multiple interconnected modules in the codebase. This motivated us to propose the Flow-Graph-Driven Multi-Agent Framework (FGDM), which is composed of four agents that operate in a sequential manner. The framework converts the received code to a flow graph, identifies the erroneous segments, and further generates the repaired code. All the employed agents utilize Chain-of-Thought (COT) and Tree-of-Thoughts (TOT) prompts. Additionally, we also integrated with the FAISS vector database to retrieve similar previous bugs and their repairs. We demonstrated the efficacy of the proposed framework over 100 programs from several projects, including Ansible, Black, FastAPI, Keras, Luigi, Matplotlib, Pandas, Scrapy, SpaCy, and Tornado in both C and Python programs. Our experiments demonstrate that the FGDM outperforms the extant approaches and yielded reductions with a mean of 24.33 and 8.37 in Levenshtein distance and similarities of 0.951 and 0.974 in cosine similarity for Python and C, respectively.

</details>


### [136] [Supporting Belonging in Software Engineering Through Role Models Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25099)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究采用自我民族志方法，探讨将历史上计算机先驱融入日常软件工程教学的有效性。通过反思备忘录和教学 artifacts 分析，表明这一低干扰的教学实践能够在不改变学习目标和评估的前提下，将代表性融入技术内容，实现包容性与技术严谨性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Role models 在教育研究中被认为对学生身份认同和归属感具有重要影响，但如何在日常工程教学中系统性地嵌入 role model 的可见性尚未得到充分关注。

Method: 分析性自我民族志方法（analytic autoethnographic approach），基于多轮课程教学中的反思备忘录和教学 artifacts，表征如何将历史先驱的简短、主题对齐的 contextualization 融入核心技术 lectures。

Result: 这种结构性嵌入的方法被证明是一种低干扰的教学实践，将代表性表述与学科内容对齐，将多元化贡献者定位为软件架构发展的基础。整合过程是迭代的，经过多学期优化以加强主题对齐和教学流畅性。

Conclusion: 在技术内容中嵌入历史性代表性可能是支持包容性同时保持工程教育技术严谨性的实用机制。

Abstract: Role models are widely discussed in educational research as influential in students identity development and sense of belonging, yet less attention has been given to how role model visibility can be systematically embedded within everyday engineering instruction. This paper presents an analytic autoethnographic account of integrating historically grounded role models into routine software engineering teaching practice. Drawing on reflective memos and instructional artifacts across multiple course offerings, we characterize how brief, topic aligned contextualizations of pioneers were incorporated into core technical lectures without altering learning objectives or assessments. The findings indicate that this structurally embedded approach functioned as a low disruption pedagogical practice that aligned representation with disciplinary substance, situating diverse contributors as foundational to the development of software architecture. The integration was iterative and refined across semesters to strengthen topic alignment and instructional flow. These results suggest that embedding historically grounded representation within technical content may serve as a practical mechanism for supporting inclusivity while preserving technical rigor in engineering education.

</details>


### [137] [R$^3$-SQL: Ranking Reward and Resampling for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25325)
*Hojae Han,Yeonseok Jeong,Seung-won Hwang,Zhewei Yao,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: R³-SQL是一个改进Text-to-SQL系统的新框架，通过统一奖励机制解决功能等价SQL查询评分不一致的问题，并通过智能重采样策略改善候选池质量，在BIRD-dev上达到75.03%的执行准确率，创使用公开规模模型的最高记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL系统存在两个关键缺陷：1) 功能等价的SQL查询（执行结果相同）评分不一致；2) 当正确SQL不在候选池时，排序方法无法恢复正确结果。这两个问题限制了系统的可靠性和实用性。

Method: R³-SQL采用双管齐下的方法。首先，通过统一奖励机制进行排序：将候选SQL按执行结果分组，保证功能等价查询获得一致评分；结合组间成对偏好与组内点式效用（考虑最佳组排名和大小）来评估每个分组。其次，引入智能重采样（agentic resampling）：评估当前候选池质量，在可能缺失正确SQL时主动触发重采样。

Result: 在BIRD-dev基准上达到75.03%的执行准确率，在五个基准测试中均获得一致提升，成为使用公开规模模型的方法中的最新最优水平。

Conclusion: 通过将一致性约束纳入排序机制，并结合智能重采样策略改善候选池召回率，R³-SQL有效解决了现有方法的两个核心局限。该框架具有良好通用性，可广泛应用于需要候选生成与排序的神经符号推理任务。

Abstract: Modern Text-to-SQL systems generate multiple candidate SQL queries and rank them to judge a final prediction. However, existing methods face two limitations. First, they often score functionally equivalent SQL queries inconsistently despite identical execution results. Second, ranking cannot recover when the correct SQL is absent from the candidate pool. We propose R$^3$-SQL, a Text-to-SQL framework that addresses both issues through unified reward for ranking and resampling. R$^3$-SQL first groups candidates by execution result and ranks groups for consistency. To score each group, it combines a pairwise preference across groups with a pointwise utility from the best group rank and size, capturing relative preference, consistency, and candidate quality. To improve candidate recall, R$^3$-SQL introduces agentic resampling, which judges the generated candidate pool and selectively resamples when the correct SQL is likely absent. R$^3$-SQL achieves 75.03 execution accuracy on BIRD-dev, a new state of the art among methods using models with disclosed sizes, with consistent gains across five benchmarks.

</details>


### [138] [Commit-Aware Learning-Based Test Case Prioritization for Continuous Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25363)
*Lorenzo Abbondante,Gerardo Canfora*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合代码变更结构信息、测试覆盖关系和历史执行行为的提交感知学习模型用于CI环境中的测试用例优先排序，有效提升回归故障检测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI流水线中的回归测试因测试套件规模增长和执行频率增加而成本日益增高，现有技术主要依赖历史执行数据和覆盖度量，忽略了代码变更中丰富的结构信息。

Method: 提出了一种提交感知的基于学习的TCP方法，将版本控制diff的结构属性、测试覆盖关系和历史执行行为整合到统一预测模型中，给定新提交后估计每个测试用例揭示至少一个故障的概率，并据此优先执行测试。

Result: 在五个Defects4J项目上使用留一项目跨项目验证设置进行评估，提交感知的TCP在分类和优先排序有效性方面均显著优于非提交感知基线。

Conclusion: 包含提交结构语义显著提升了回归故障检测能力，并在CI环境中实现了稳健、可泛化的基于学习的测试用例优先排序。

Abstract: Regression testing in Continuous Integration (CI) pipelines is increasingly costly due to the growing size and execution frequency of test suites. Test Case Prioritization (TCP) mitigates this problem by reordering tests to expose faults earlier. However, most existing techniques rely primarily on historical execution data and coverage metrics, neglecting the rich structural information contained in code changes. This paper proposes a commit-aware, learning-based TCP method that combines structural properties of version-control diffs, test coverage relations, and historical execution behavior into a unified predictive model. Given a new commit, the method estimates the probability that each test suite will reveal at least one failure and prioritizes test execution accordingly. We evaluate our method on five Defects4J projects using a leave-one-project-out cross-project validation setting. Results show that the commit-aware TCP significantly outperform non-commit-aware-baselines in both classification and prioritization effectiveness. Our findings show that including commit structural semantics substantially enhances regression fault detection and enables robust, generalizable learning-based TCP in CI environments.

</details>


### [139] [CoRE: A Fine-Grained Code Reasoning Benchmark Beyond Output Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25399)
*Jun Gao,Yun Peng,Qian Qiao,Changhai Zhou,Yuhua Zhou,Shiyang Zhang,Shichao Weng,Zhenchang Xing,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRE基准测试，通过实现不变性（implementation invariance）和过程透明性（process transparency）两个维度评估LLM的代码推理能力。实验揭示当前模型存在"鲁棒性差距"和"表面执行"两大问题，表明仅评估最终输出正确性不足以衡量真实的代码推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在代码生成任务上表现优异，但其是否真正进行代码推理尚不明确。现有基准测试主要在单一标准实现下评估最终输出正确性，忽略了两个关键问题：(1)模型能否在功能等价的实现间保持一致性；(2)模型能否准确推理中间执行状态。

Method: 提出CoRE（Code Reasoning benchmark）基准测试，通过两个核心维度评估代码推理能力：1）实现不变性：测试模型对功能等价但实现不同的代码的推理一致性；2）过程透明性：验证模型对中间执行状态的推理准确性。对8个前沿LLM进行了广泛评估。

Result: 发现两个关键局限：(1)鲁棒性差距：模型在不同等价实现间性能差异显著；(2)表面执行：模型能得出正确答案却未正确推理中间执行状态。

Conclusion: 仅评估输出正确性的方法不足以衡量代码推理能力，CoRE作为评估鲁棒且忠实代码推理的必要基准。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on code generation tasks, it remains unclear whether large language models (LLMs) genuinely reason about code execution. Existing code reasoning benchmarks primarily evaluate final output correctness under a single canonical implementation, leaving two critical aspects underexplored: (1) whether LLMs can maintain consistency to functionally equivalent implementations, and (2) whether LLMs can accurately reason about intermediate execution states. We introduce \textbf{CoRE}, a \textbf{Co}de \textbf{Re}asoning benchmark that evaluates code reasoning through \textbf{implementation invariance} and \textbf{process transparency}. Extensive evaluations on eight frontier LLMs reveal two fundamental limitations. First, models exhibit a substantial \textbf{robustness gap}, with performance varying significantly across equivalent implementations. Second, we observe \textbf{superficial execution}, where models arrive at correct final outputs without correctly reasoning about intermediate execution states. Together, these findings demonstrate that output-only evaluations are insufficient for assessing code reasoning and position CoRE as a necessary benchmark for evaluating robust and faithful code reasoning.\footnote{Data and code are available at https://github.com/ZJUSig/CoRE.}

</details>


### [140] [Recommending Usability Improvements with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25420)
*Sebastian Lubos,Alexander Felfernig,Damian Garber,Viet-Man Le,Manuel Henrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的自动化可用性评估方法，通过分析应用上下文和用户交互的屏幕录制视频，自动基于Nielsen可用性启发式准则识别可用性问题并生成改进建议，按严重程度排序，旨在降低可用性评估的专业门槛和资源需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可用性评估方法需要大量专业知识和资源，对小型团队和组织而言具有挑战性。随着多模态大语言模型的出现，可以处理图像和视频等视觉输入，为自动化可用性评估和生成改进建议提供了新机遇。

Method: 采用受控的应用程序上下文和用户交互屏幕录制作为MLLM输入；基于Nielsen可用性启发式准则自动识别和描述可用性问题；为减少开发者手动优先级排序的工作量，按严重程度对建议进行排序。

Result: 用户研究结果表明，该方法具有为软件开发人员提供低工作量可用性改进建议的潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法是传统评估方法的有价值补充，尤其适用于可用性专家资源有限的环境，可作为未来集成到开发工具中的基础，实现软件工程工作流程内的自动化可用性评估。

Abstract: Usability describes quality attributes of application user interfaces that determine how effectively users can interact with them. Traditional usability evaluation methods require considerable expertise and resources, which can be challenging, especially for small teams and organizations. Automating usability evaluation could make it more accessible and help to improve the user experience. The recent emergence of powerful multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has opened new opportunities for automating usability evaluation and recommendation of improvements. These models can process visual inputs such as images and videos alongside textual context, which enables the identification of usability issues and the generation of actionable suggestions to resolve these issues.
  In this paper, we present a novel automated approach that uses limited application context and screen recordings of user interactions as input to an MLLM. The model automatically identifies and describes usability issues based on Nielsens usability heuristics, and provides corresponding explanations and improvement recommendations. To reduce the developer effort of manual prioritization, the recommendations are ranked by severity. The quality and practical usefulness of the generated recommendations were evaluated based on a user study that involved software engineers as participants. The evaluation focused on the highest-ranked suggestions provided by the model. The results demonstrate the potential of our approach to provide low-effort usability improvement recommendations. This makes it a promising complement to traditional evaluation methods, especially in settings with limited access to usability experts. In this sense, the approach serves as a basis for future integration into development tools to enable automated usability evaluation within software engineering workflows.

</details>


### [141] [AI as Consumer and Participant: A Co-Design Agenda for MBSE Substrates and Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25526)
*Siyuan Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI tools are being deployed over MBSE models today, and those models were not designed for this kind of consumption. The problem is not simply that tools hallucinate: well-prompted frontier models produce competent, useful output over a conformant SysML model, but the reasoning they produce is drawn from training rather than retrieved from the model itself, and different tools over the same model produce different results with nothing in the record to adjudicate between them. The model, in other words, is functioning as a prompt rather than as a knowledge base. Attaching better tools to the same model does not resolve this. The model and the methodology that governs its construction need to be designed together for AI participation, treating the model as a machine-queryable knowledge substrate rather than a structured artefact for human navigation, and that co-design has not yet happened in any systematic way. This paper works through a concrete workflow scenario to show what that gap looks like in practice, proposes three principles that jointly characterise what model and methodology must achieve together, and closes with a call to the community to begin this work before the architectural decisions about AI integration settle without the methodological foundation they require.

</details>


### [142] [Using Large Language Models for Black-Box Testing of FMU-Based Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25650)
*Abdullah Mughees,Gaadha Sudheerbabu,Tanwir Ahmad,Dragos Truscan,Mikael Manngård,Kristian Klemets*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"人在回路"的方法，利用大语言模型（LLM）辅助黑盒测试功能模拟单元（FMU）。LLM根据FMU的功能和接口规范生成结构化的测试场景目标（Given-When-Then格式），自动生成输入时间序列，运行仿真并评估断言，最终输出人类可读的日志和可视化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态仿真模型的测试场景定义需要大量人工工作，且测试结果的可解释性不足。传统的FMU黑盒测试缺乏自动化和可解释性手段，需要一种能够自动生成测试场景并提供清晰结果展示的方法。

Method: 1) 输入FMU的功能和接口规范；2) 利用LLM生成结构化的场景目标（Given-When-Then格式），定义初始输入条件、条件变化和预期输出行为；3) 生成对应的场景计划，包括输入模式和断言预言；4) 生成完整的输入时间序列并运行FMU仿真；5) 对记录输出进行断言评估；6) 生成人类可读的日志、可视化图表和统计结果。

Result: 在Lube Oil Cooling（润滑油冷却）系统上验证了方法的有效性。结果表明LLM辅助的场景生成能够促进动态仿真模型的自动测试设计和验证，并讨论了使该方法适合日常使用的设计选择。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的测试场景生成方法可以显著减少动态仿真模型测试的人工工作量，提高测试结果的可解释性。该方法在实际应用中具有可行性，能够支持工程师在日常工作中更高效地进行FMU验证。

Abstract: We propose a human in the loop approach for black-box testing of Functional Mock-up Units (FMUs) using Large Language Models (LLMs). The goal is to reduce the manual effort in defining test scenarios for dynamic simulation models and to improve the interpretability of results. The approach takes the functional and interface specifications of an FMU as input, and prompts an LLM to generate structured scenario goals in Given-When-Then format that define the initial input conditions of the simulation, a possible change in those conditions, and the expected output behaviour of the system against those changes. The corresponding scenario plans specify input patterns and add assertion oracles that describe expected output patterns defined in scenario goals. The approach generates a complete input time series for the scenario plans, runs the FMU simulation, and evaluates assertions on the recorded outputs. It produces human-readable logs and plots that show statistics for each scenario with overlays, aggregate pass rates, and per-goal outcomes. The generated scenarios and results are stored for evaluation and later re-execution. We evaluate the approach on a Lube Oil Cooling system and discuss design choices that make the approach practical for everyday use. Results suggest that LLM-assisted scenario generation can facilitate automatic test design and verification of dynamic simulation models.

</details>


### [143] [Spreadsheet Modeling Experiments Using GPTs on Small Problem Statements and the Wall Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25689)
*Thomas A. Grossman,Yuan Chen,Sopiko Datuashvili*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文评估了GPT工具在构建可重用分析电子表格模型中的表现。研究筛选五个GPT扩展后选定Excel AI进行测试，评估其对ERFR标准的满足程度。结果表明GPT工具能生成结构良好的模型，但一致性和可重现性不足，识别出"信心问题"和"工作流问题"两大挑战，表明当前工具仍需熟练用户验证，当前尚不可用于专业场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GPT工具是否能辅助构建可重用的分析性电子表格模型，旨在评估其在电子表格开发中的实际应用价值。

Method: 筛选五个GPT扩展后，选择Excel AI (pulsrai.com)进行详细测试，通过简单问题陈述进行结构化实验，评估其是否满足ERFR标准（每个输入在单元格中、单元格公式、无硬编码数字、标签、准确性）。

Result: Excel AI虽能生成结构良好的模型，但表现不一致且往往不可重现。识别出两个核心挑战——"信心问题"和"工作流问题"，凸显了熟练用户验证和调整GPT生成电子表格的必要性。

Conclusion: 虽然GPT工具在生成电子表格模型草稿方面有潜力，可能减少开发时间或降低技能要求，但当前工具仍不可靠，不适合专业使用。未来研究方向包括提示工程、可重现性和大规模建模任务。

Abstract: This paper investigates how GPT-based tools can assist in building reusable analytical spreadsheet models. After a screening, we evaluate five GPT extensions and select Excel AI by pulsrai.com for detailed testing. Through structured experiments on simple problem statements, we assess Excel AI's performance against the ERFR criteria (each input in a cell; cell formulas; no hardwired numbers; labels; accurate). Results show that while Excel AI can produce well-structured models, it is inconsistent and often non-reproducible. We identify two central challenges - "the problem of confidence" and "the problem of workflow" - which highlight the need for skilled users to verify and adapt GPT-generated spreadsheets. Though GPTs show promise for generating draft models that may reduce development time or lower skill requirements, current tools remain unreliable for professional use. We conclude with recommendations for future research into prompt engineering, reproducibility, and larger-scale modeling tasks.

</details>


### [144] [Bug-Report-Driven Fault Localization: Industrial Benchmarking and Lesson Learned at ABB Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25700)
*Pernilla Hall,Anton Ununger,Riccardo Rubei,Alessio Bucaioni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了仅使用自然语言Bug报告内容进行软件故障定位的可行性，对比了传统机器学习模型（TF-IDF + 逻辑回归/SVM/随机森林）与微调Transformer模型（RoBERTa/DistilRoBERTa）在ABB工业机器人5年真实Bug报告数据集上的表现。结果传统模型反而优于Transformer模型，挑战了Transformer在工业领域"通用最优"的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中大型长生命周期系统缺陷定位成本高、维护阶段开发者只能依赖文本Bug报告而非完整运行时/代码上下文，亟需低成本、可直接部署的AI辅助调试工具。

Method: 将故障定位建模为监督文本分类问题；使用TF-IDF特征提取结合三类传统模型，以及预训练RoBERTa微调；对ABB Robotics五年历史Bug报告数据集进行评估；尝试数据增强提升随机森林性能。

Result: TF-IDF特征的传统机器学习模型在该工业数据集上持续优于微调Transformer模型；数据增强改善了随机森林的表现。

Conclusion: 基于历史Bug报告的文本分类方法可有效支持工业故障定位；传统方法在此类领域特定数据上仍具优势，Transformer并非所有场景的最优选择；该方案可低成本、可扩展地集成到现有工业维护流程中。

Abstract: Software quality assurance remains a major challenge in industrial environments, where large-scale and long-lived systems inevitably accumulate defects. Identifying the location of a fault is often time-consuming and costly, particularly during maintenance phases when developers must rely primarily on textual bug reports rather than complete runtime or code-level context. In this study, we investigated if artificial intelligence can support fault localization using only the natural-language content of bug reports. By relying only on textual information, our approach requires no access to source code, execution traces, or static analysis artifacts, making it directly deployable within existing industrial maintenance workflows. We framed fault localization as a supervised text classification problem and evaluated three traditional machine learning models (Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine, and Random Forest) and two fine-tuned transformer-based language models (RoBERTa-Base and Distil-RoBERTa). Our evaluation used proprietary data from ABB Robotics in Sweden, comprising five years of resolved industrial bug reports, each linked to its verified code fix. This setting allowed us to assess model effectiveness under realistic industrial constraints.
  Our results showed that traditional models using term frequency-inverse document features consistently outperformed the fine-tuned language models on this dataset, while data augmentation improved Random Forest performance. These findings challenge the assumption that transformer-based models universally outperform classical approaches in industrial contexts with domain-specific data. We demonstrated that historical bug reports can be systematically used for text-based, artificial intelligence-assisted fault localization, providing a scalable, low-cost, and empirically grounded complement to common debugging practices in industry.

</details>


### [145] [Learning Generalizable Multimodal Representations for Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25711)
*Zeming Dong,Yuejun Guo,Qiang Hu,Yao Zhang,Maxime Cordy,Hao Liu,Mike Papadakis,Yongqiang Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出MultiVul，一种利用代码-注释双模态对比学习进行漏洞检测的框架，通过双相似性学习和一致性正则化对齐两种模态的表示，在4个大语言模型和2个数据集上验证了方法的有效性，F1提升最高达27.07%。 | 该论文提出MultiVul，一种利用代码-注释双模态对比学习进行漏洞检测的框架，通过双相似性学习和一致性正则化对齐两种模态的表示，在4个大语言模型和2个数据集上验证了方法的有效性，F1提升最高达27.07%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞检测方法主要依赖单模态代码表示，未能充分利用代码注释中蕴含的开发者意图等补充语义信息，导致模型在处理复杂代码结构和逻辑关系时泛化能力受限。代码与注释作为互补的天然对齐模态，前者编码结构逻辑，后者捕获开发者意图，二者结合可提升漏洞检测效果。 | 现有漏洞检测方法主要依赖单模态代码表示，未能充分利用代码注释中蕴含的开发者意图等补充语义信息，导致模型在处理复杂代码结构和逻辑关系时泛化能力受限。代码与注释作为互补的天然对齐模态，前者编码结构逻辑，后者捕获开发者意图，二者结合可提升漏洞检测效果。

Method: MultiVul是一个多模态对比学习框架，核心机制包括：1）双相似性学习（dual similarity learning）实现代码与注释表示的对齐；2）一致性正则化（consistency regularization）增强模态对齐的稳定性；3）通过多样化的代码-文本配对数据增强模型鲁棒性。该框架可在多种LLM上实现，包括DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B、Qwen2.5-Coder-7B、StarCoder2-7B和CodeLlama-7B。 | MultiVul是一个多模态对比学习框架，核心机制包括：1）双相似性学习（dual similarity learning）实现代码与注释表示的对齐；2）一致性正则化（consistency regularization）增强模态对齐的稳定性；3）通过多样化的代码-文本配对数据增强模型鲁棒性。该框架可在多种LLM上实现，包括DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B、Qwen2.5-Coder-7B、StarCoder2-7B和CodeLlama-7B。

Result: 在DiverseVul和Devign两个主流数据集上，MultiVul相比基于提示的方法最高提升27.07%的F1分数，相比仅使用代码的微调方法提升13.37%，同时保持相近的推理效率。实验覆盖4个主流代码大语言模型，验证了方法的通用性和有效性。 | 在DiverseVul和Devign两个主流数据集上，MultiVul相比基于提示的方法最高提升27.07%的F1分数，相比仅使用代码的微调方法提升13.37%，同时保持相近的推理效率。实验覆盖4个主流代码大语言模型，验证了方法的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: MultiVul通过双模态对齐和一致性正则化，显著提升了基于LLM的漏洞检测性能，在保持推理效率的同时，F1指标最高提升27.07%。该方法验证了代码-注释互补信息在漏洞检测中的价值。实验覆盖DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B、Qwen2.5-Coder-7B、StarCoder2-7B和CodeLlama-7B四个大模型，在DiverseVul和Devign数据集上均表现优异。 | MultiVul通过双模态对齐和一致性正则化，显著提升了基于LLM的漏洞检测性能，在保持推理效率的同时，F1指标最高提升27.07%。该方法验证了代码-注释互补信息在漏洞检测中的价值。实验覆盖DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B、Qwen2.5-Coder-7B、StarCoder2-7B和CodeLlama-7B四个大模型，在DiverseVul和Devign数据集上均表现优异。

Abstract: Source code and its accompanying comments are complementary yet naturally aligned modalities-code encodes structural logic while comments capture developer intent. However, existing vulnerability detection methods mostly rely on single-modality code representations, overlooking the complementary semantic information embedded in comments and thus limiting their generalization across complex code structures and logical relationships. To address this, we propose MultiVul, a multimodal contrastive framework that aligns code and comment representations through dual similarity learning and consistency regularization, augmented with diverse code-text pairs to improve robustness. Experiments on widely adopted DiverseVul and Devign datasets across four large language models (LLMs) (i.e., DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B, StarCoder2-7B, and CodeLlama-7B) show that MultiVul achieves up to 27.07% F1 improvement over prompting-based methods and 13.37% over code-only Fine-Tuning, while maintaining comparable inference efficiency.

</details>


### [146] [Can Code Evaluation Metrics Detect Code Plagiarism?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25778)
*Fahad Ebrahim,Mike Joy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对比实验评估了5种代码评估指标在源代码抄袭检测中的表现，发现CrystalBLEU等CEMs在预处理后性能可超越专业检测工具，且在复杂修改等级（L6）上仍保持竞争力，证明了CEMs作为抄袭检测工具的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码抄袭检测（SCPD）和代码评估指标（CEMs）已被广泛研究，但CEMs在检测不同修改复杂度等级（L1-L6）抄袭方面的可靠性尚未明确。

Method: 采用无阈值排序评估方法，在两个开源标注数据集（ConPlag和IRPlag）上评估5种CEMs，与两种SOTA工具进行对比实验，并分析预处理效果。

Result: 无预处理时Dolos整体表现最优，CrystalBLEU/CodeBLEU/RUBY优于JPlag；L1性能最强，L4后性能下降，但CrystalBLEU在L6仍具竞争力。预处理后CrystalBLEU超越Dolos；ConPlag raw数据集上Dolos最佳，其余数据集上CrystalBLEU领先。

Conclusion: CEMs（CrystalBLEU、CodeBLEU、RUBY等）在排序指标上与专用SCPD工具（JPlag、Dolos）具有可比性，可作为代码相似性检测的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Source Code Plagiarism Detection (SCPD) plays an important role in maintaining fairness and academic integrity in software engineering education. Code Evaluation Metrics (CEMs) are developed for assessing code generation tasks. However, it remains unclear whether such metrics can reliably detect plagiarism across different levels of modification (L1-L6), increasing in complexity.
  In this paper, we perform a comparative empirical study using two open-source labelled datasets, ConPlag (raw and template-free versions) and IRPlag. We evaluate five CEMs, namely CodeBLEU, CrystalBLEU, RUBY, Tree Structured Edit Distance (TSED), and CodeBERTScore. The performance is evaluated using threshold-free ranking-based measures to assess overall, per dataset, and per-level plagiarism performance. The results are compared against state-of-the-art (SOTA) Source Code Plagiarism Detection Tools (SCPDTs), JPlag and Dolos.
  Our findings show that without preprocessing, Dolos achieves the highest overall ranking performance, while among the individual metrics, CrystalBLEU, CodeBLEU, and RUBY outperform JPlag. Performance is strongest at L1 and drops from L4 onward, while CrystalBLEU remains competitive on L6. With preprocessing, CrystalBLEU surpasses Dolos overall. Per dataset, Dolos achieved the best ranking on the ConPlag raw dataset, while CrystalBLEU was the best-performing metric on the remaining datasets. At the plagiarism levels, Dolos remains strongest on L4, while Crystal-BLEU leads most of the remaining difficult levels. These results indicate that CEMs are comparable to dedicated tools in terms of ranking metrics.

</details>


### [147] [SAFEdit: Does Multi-Agent Decomposition Resolve the Reliability Challenges of Instructed Code Editing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25737)
*Noam Tarshish,Nofar Selouk,Daniel Hodisan,Bar Ezra Gafniel,Yuval Elovici,Asaf Shabtai,Eliya Nachmani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SAFEdit是一个多智能体框架，通过Planner、Editor和Verifier的协作，以及Failure Abstraction Layer实现的迭代优化，在EditBench上达到68.6%的任务成功率，显著优于单智能体方法，并有效减少了指令级幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在指令引导的代码编辑任务上表现不佳，EditBench基准测试中40个模型里有39个任务成功率低于60%，说明通用代码生成与可执行测试约束下的指令驱动编辑之间存在显著差距。

Method: SAFEdit框架将代码编辑过程分解为三个专业化角色：(1) Planner Agent生成显式、可见性感知的编辑计划；(2) Editor Agent执行最小化、字面性的代码修改；(3) Verifier Agent执行真实测试。当测试失败时，Failure Abstraction Layer (FAL)将原始测试日志转换为结构化诊断反馈，反馈给Editor进行迭代优化。

Result: 在5种语言(英/波/西/中/俄)、445个代码编辑实例上评估，SAFEdit达到68.6%任务成功率，比单模型基线提升3.8个百分点，比ReAct单智能体基线提升8.6个百分点。迭代优化循环贡献了17.4个百分点的成功率提升。

Conclusion: SAFEdit有效减少了指令级幻觉，其自动化错误分析能力为理解失败原因提供了超越通过/失败二元结果的解释框架。

Abstract: Instructed code editing is a significant challenge for large language models (LLMs). On the EditBench benchmark, 39 of 40 evaluated models obtain a task success rate (TSR) below 60 percent, highlighting a gap between general code generation and the ability to perform instruction-driven editing under executable test constraints. To address this, we propose SAFEdit, a multi-agent framework for instructed code editing that decomposes the editing process into specialized roles to improve reliability and reduce unintended code changes. A Planner Agent produces an explicit, visibility-aware edit plan, an Editor Agent applies minimal, literal code modifications, and a Verifier Agent executes real test runs. When tests fail, SAFEdit uses a Failure Abstraction Layer (FAL) to transform raw test logs into structured diagnostic feedback, which is fed back to the Editor to support iterative refinement. We compare SAFEdit against both prior single-model results reported for EditBench and an implemented ReAct single-agent baseline under the same evaluation conditions. We used EditBench to evaluate SAFEdit on 445 code editing instances in five languages (English, Polish, Spanish, Chinese, and Russian) under varying spatial context variants. SAFEdit achieved 68.6 percent TSR, outperforming the single-model baseline by 3.8 percentage points and the ReAct single-agent baseline by 8.6 percentage points. The iterative refinement loop was found to contribute 17.4 percentage points to SAFEdit's overall success rate. SAFEdit's automated error analysis further indicates a reduction in instruction-level hallucinations compared to single-agent approaches, providing an additional framework component for interpreting failures beyond pass or fail outcomes.

</details>


### [148] [Scenario-based System Testing for Distributed Robotics Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25772)
*Jan Peleska,Felix Brüning,Wen-Ling Huang,Anne E. Haxthausen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present the SCenario Specification Language (SCSL) for automated generation and execution of system-level tests. SCSL targets complex distributed systems (e.g., collaborating autonomous robots) where classical model-based testing becomes impractical because (1) the overall system complexity is too high for a single monolithic model, (2) test behaviour cannot be fully precomputed due to substantial nondeterminism in the distributed system under test (SUT), and (3) the SUT configuration may change dynamically at runtime. Challenge (1) is addressed by scenarios: each scenario specifies test-specific expected SUT behaviour and/or stimuli to be applied during execution. Complex system tests are composed from elementary scenarios using sequential and parallel composition. To address (2), the SCSL tool platform supports online (on-the-fly) testing, selecting and executing test steps during runtime. For (3), SCSL provides a collaboration construct that supports dynamic reconfiguration: removing unavailable components, registering newly joining components, and rewiring interfaces during test execution. We illustrate the syntax and semantics of SCSL using a system-test example in which robots perform a salvage mission, and we use an automatically generated test execution to demonstrate the concepts supported by our prototype tool platform.

</details>


### [149] [Key Developer Roles and Organizational Coupling in Microservices: A Longitudinal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25804)
*Xiaozhou Li,Nariman Mani,Jose Sosa Rodriguez,Tomas Cerny*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This study examines how developer roles (Jacks-broad knowledge holders, Mavens-deep specialists, Connectors-organizational bridges) contribute to organizational coupling in large-scale microservice systems. Through longitudinal GitHub data analysis, researchers found that Connectors drive higher coupling, multi-role developers amplify coupling effects, while Jacks and Mavens have more localized influences. Organizational coupling is thus a role-driven phenomenon, not just a structural property.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Microservice-based systems impose significant organizational coordination challenges, yet prior work largely focuses on structural architectural aspects. The role of individual developers in shaping organizational coupling remains underexplored, leaving gaps in understanding how developer roles influence coordination dynamics over time.

Method: Longitudinal repository mining analysis of GitHub data including commits, issues, and pull request interactions to operationalize organizational coupling and quantify its evolution over time.

Result: Connectors are consistently associated with higher levels of organizational coupling. The co-occurrence of multiple roles within the same developer further amplifies coupling effects. In contrast, Jacks and Mavens exhibit more localized and role-specific influences.

Conclusion: Organizational coupling in microservices is primarily a role-driven phenomenon rather than an inevitable structural property. The findings support role-aware organizational design and targeted decoupling strategies. Notably, developer co-occurrence of multiple roles amplifies coupling effects.

Abstract: Microservice-based systems impose significant organizational coordination challenges, yet the role of individual developers in shaping organizational coupling (OC) remains underexplored. Prior work largely focuses on structural architectural aspects, leaving gaps in understanding how developer roles influence coordination dynamics over time. This study investigates how different developer roles contribute to OC in a large-scale microservices system. The analysis focuses on three key roles, namely Jacks, representing broad knowledge holders, Mavens, representing deep specialists, and Connectors, representing organizational bridges. A longitudinal repository mining analysis of GitHub data, including commits and issue and pull request interactions, is conducted to operationalize OC and quantify its evolution over time. The results show that Connectors are consistently associated with higher levels of OC, while the co-occurrence of multiple roles within the same developer further amplifies coupling effects. In contrast, Jacks and Mavens exhibit more localized and role-specific influences. These findings indicate that OC in microservices is primarily a role-driven phenomenon rather than an inevitable structural property, providing a foundation for role-aware organizational design and targeted decoupling strategies.

</details>


### [150] [Does social identity matter in software engineering? Assessing the case of research software engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25831)
*Chukwudi Uwasomba,Tamara Lopez,Melanie Langer,Helen Sharp,Michel Wermelinger,Caroline Jay,Mark Levine,Bashar Nuseibeh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Social identity is a concept from psychology that refers to the part of an individual's identity that derives from their group membership(s). In this paper, we explore social identity in members of the professional community of Research Software Engineers (RSEs). Using a mixed-methods approach, our study combined computational linguistic analysis and inferential statistics to examine over 28,000 social media posts, 1,700 blogs, and survey responses from 381 professional RSEs. The findings highlight the emergence of a collective RSE identity and demonstrate its role in shaping professional wellbeing. This study contributes an interdisciplinary perspective by integrating social psychology and software engineering to show how a professional identity evolves and why it matters.

</details>


### [151] [RESTestBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating the Effectiveness of LLM-Generated REST API Test Cases from NL Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25862)
*Leon Kogler,Stefan Hangler,Maximilian Ehrhart,Benedikt Dornauer,Roland Wuersching,Peter Schrammel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RESTestBench是一个用于评估REST API NL需求测试生成的基准，引入了需求驱动变异测试度量。研究发现当生成器与错误/变异代码交互时测试效果显著下降，尤其对于模糊需求，说明需求详细程度比SUT反馈更具价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional metrics (code coverage, crash-based fault detection) are inadequate proxies for validating functional behavior when LLMs generate tests from NL requirements, necessitating requirement-based evaluation approaches.

Method: Built RESTestBench benchmark with 3 REST services and NL requirements in precise/vague variants; introduced requirements-based mutation testing metric; conducted comparative evaluation of non-refinement vs refinement-based test generation across multiple LLMs.

Result: Test effectiveness significantly degrades when generators interact with faulty/mutated code, particularly for vague requirements—sometimes negating refinement benefits. SUT interaction is unnecessary when requirement detail is high.

Conclusion: For LLM-based test generation from NL requirements, detailed requirements are more valuable than SUT interaction for ensuring test effectiveness, rendering SUT feedback unnecessary when requirement precision is high.

Abstract: Existing REST API testing tools are typically evaluated using code coverage and crash-based fault metrics. However, recent LLM-based approaches increasingly generate tests from NL requirements to validate functional behaviour, making traditional metrics weak proxies for whether generated tests validate intended behaviour. To address this gap, we present RESTestBench, a benchmark comprising three REST services paired with manually verified NL requirements in both precise and vague variants, enabling controlled and reproducible evaluation of requirement-based test generation. RESTestBench further introduces a requirements-based mutation testing metric that measures the fault-detection effectiveness of a generated test case with respect to a specific requirement, extending the property-based approach of Bartocci et al. . Using RESTestBench, we evaluate two approaches across multiple state-of-the-art LLMs: (i) non-refinement-based generation, and (ii) refinement-based generation guided by interaction with the running SUT. In the refinement experiments, RESTestBench assesses how exposure to the actual implementation, valid or mutated, affects test effectiveness. Our results show that test effectiveness drops considerably when the generator interacts with faulty or mutated code, especially for vague requirements, sometimes negating the benefit of refinement and indicating that incorporating actual SUT behaviour is unnecessary when requirement detail is high.

</details>


### [152] [From Threads to Trajectories: A Multi-LLM Pipeline for Community Knowledge Extraction from GitHub Issue Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25880)
*Nazia Shehnaz Joynab,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-MIMIC-Bench，一个基于多LLM自动化流水线从GitHub讨论中生成的问题轨迹数据集，用于帮助理解和解决大型开源软件项目中的复杂issue。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型开源软件项目的问题解决过程需要开发者投入大量认知努力，因为需要梳理冗长、非结构化且碎片化的issue讨论线程，这严重阻碍了问题诊断效率。

Method: 开发了一个多LLM流水线，利用五个闭源LLM配置执行细粒度任务：标签分类、内联代码块和外部链接摘要、评论分析、标签特定字段分类以及轨迹合成。流水线分析评论时具有外部链接资源意识，将评论分类到标签特定字段（如根因、解决方案、实施进度），并合成标签感知轨迹以捕捉整个讨论的结构化叙事。

Result: 在800个来自SWE-Bench-Pro、SWE-Bench-Multilingual和SWE-Bench-Verified的真实GitHub issues上评估，成功率达到91.7%，提取出734条高保真推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 该系统可辅助开发者社区理解经验驱动的协作式问题诊断方法，生成的轨迹还可用于训练现代LLM智能体，使其具备像专家开发者一样思考和行动的能力。

Abstract: Resolution of complex post-production issues in large-scale open-source software (OSS) projects requires significant cognitive effort, as developers need to go through long, unstructured and fragmented issue discussion threads before that. In this paper, we present SWE-MIMIC-Bench, an issue trajectory dataset generated from raw GitHub discussions using an automated multi-LLM pipeline. Unlike simple summarization, this pipeline utilizes a group of closed-source LLMs to perform granular tasks: analyzing individual comments with awareness of externally-linked resources, classifying comment analyses into label-specific fields (e.g., root cause, solution plan, implementation progress), and synthesizing label-aware trajectories which capture a structured and coherent narrative of the entire discussion thread. Our pipeline uses five closed-source LLM configurations for distinct purposes: label classification, inline code block and external link summarization, comment analysis, label-specific field classification and trajectory synthesis. By generating concise and reliable trajectories from complex conversation threads, this system can assist developers and researchers of broader software engineering community to understand the experience-driven collaborative approach for issue diagnosis. Furthermore, the generated trajectories can be used to train modern LLM agents to think and act like an expert developer. We evaluated our system on 800 real-world GitHub issues drawn from the SWE-Bench-Pro, SWE-Bench-Multilingual and SWE-Bench-Verified dataset, achieving a 91.7% success rate in extracting 734 high-fidelity reasoning trajectories.

</details>


### [153] [Carbon-Taxed Transformers: A Green Compression Pipeline for Overgrown Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25903)
*Ajmain Inqiad Alam,Palash Roy,Chanchal K. Roy,Banani Roy,Kevin A. Schneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Carbon-Taxed Transformers (CTT)，一种受碳税经济原理启发的多架构压缩流程，通过对计算资源征税来惩罚架构低效、奖励压缩优化。在代码克隆检测、代码摘要和代码生成三个任务上，CTT实现了高达49倍的内存压缩、8-10倍的时间加速、81%的碳排放降低，同时保持约98%的克隆检测准确率、89%的摘要准确率和91%的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程中的广泛应用带来了隐性危机：不可持续的计算成本。这些模型虽性能出色，但规模过大、部署缓慢、内存密集、碳排放高。这不仅威胁AI驱动SE的可扩展性和可访问性，更影响长期环境可持续性。传统方法仅关注准确性，忽视了效率和环保成本，需要将效率与环保作为核心设计约束。

Method: 本文提出Carbon-Taxed Transformers (CTT)——一种受经济碳税原则启发的系统性多架构压缩流程。CTT将计算碳税概念操作化，对架构低效施加惩罚，对部署就绪的压缩给予奖励。方法在三种模型架构（纯编码器、编码器-解码器、解码器）上评估。

Result: CTT在三个核心SE任务上取得显著成果：(1)内存压缩高达49倍；(2)时间加速：克隆检测8-10倍、摘要3倍、生成4-7倍；(3)碳排放降低达81%；(4)精度保持：克隆检测约98%、摘要约89%、生成文本指标91%、pass@1达68%。消融实验验证了流程排序和组件贡献的必要性。

Conclusion: CTT为负责任AI在软件工程中的应用提供了可行路径，通过激进但保留性能的压缩策略，在效率和环保之间取得平衡。这项工作将效率和环境成本确立为模型设计的一等公民，为绿色AI在SE领域的可持续发展奠定基础。

Abstract: The accelerating adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) in software engineering (SE) has brought with it a silent crisis: unsustainable computational cost. While these models demonstrate remarkable capabilities in different SE tasks, they are unmanageably large, slow to deploy, memory-intensive, and carbon-heavy. This reality threatens not only the scalability and accessibility of AI-powered SE, but also its long-term environmental sustainability. The research challenge is clear: we must go beyond accuracy and address efficiency and environmental cost as first-class design constraints. To meet this challenge, we introduce Carbon-Taxed Transformers (CTT), a systematic multi-architectural compression principled pipeline ordering inspired by economic carbon taxation principles. Drawing from the economic concept of carbon pricing, CTT operationalizes a computational carbon tax that penalizes architectural inefficiencies and rewards deployment-ready compression. We evaluate CTT across three core SE tasks: code clone detection, code summarization, and code generation, with models spanning encoder-only, encoder-decoder, and decoder-only architecture. Our results show that CTT delivers on inference: (1) up to 49x memory reduction, (2) time reduction up to 8-10x for clone detection, up to 3x for summarization, and 4-7x for generation, (3) up to 81% reduction in CO2 emissions and (4) CTT retains around 98% accuracy on clone detection, around 89% on summarization, and up to 91% (textual metrics) and 68% (pass@1) for generation. Two ablation studies show that pipeline ordering and individual component contributions are both essential, providing empirical justification for CTT's design and effectiveness. This work establishes a viable path toward responsible AI in SE through aggressive yet performance-preserving compression.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [GCA-BULF: A Bottom-Up Framework for Short-Term Load Forecasting Using Grouped Critical Appliances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24766)
*Yunhao Yao,Jinwei Fang,Puhan Luo,Zhiqiang Wang,Jiahui Hou,Xiang-Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GCA-BULF框架，通过关键电器筛选、相关电器分组和协同负荷预测三个模块，在保证预测精度的前提下大幅降低电器监控成本。实验表明该方法在住宅和办公场景下均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分时电价和阶梯电价的普及，需要可靠的短期负荷预测（STLF）来支持峰值转移策略；传统自顶向下方法难以捕捉复杂用能模式，而现有自底向上方法虽能提高精度但监控所有电器的成本过高。

Method: 提出三模块框架：1）关键电器筛选模块，基于功率消耗、开关频率和使用模式周期性对电器进行排序，通过迭代负荷分解识别关键电器；2）相关电器分组模块，基于空间和时间相关性对关键电器进行聚类，实现分组级预测；3）协同负荷预测模块，整合多个分组预测结果优化总负荷预测。

Result: 在住宅和办公建筑负荷预测任务中，GCA-BULF相比现有自顶向下方法提升20.85%-57.88%，相比自底向上方法提升33.03%-92.48%。

Conclusion: GCA-BULF通过分组关键电器的方法，有效解决了传统自底向上方法中监控成本过高的问题，在住宅和办公建筑场景下均取得了显著的负荷预测提升。

Abstract: With the rise of time-of-use and tiered electricity pricing, energy consumers are encouraged to adopt peak-shifting strategies by automatically controlling high-power appliances. These help lower energy costs while enhancing the power grid's stability. To support such energy management with high resilience and responsiveness, reliable short-term load forecasting (STLF) plays a critical role. STLF predicts electricity consumption over time horizons ranging from minutes to days, using historical data, temporal patterns, and contextual factors. Traditional top-down forecasting methods struggle to capture the complex consumption patterns of diverse and mixed appliance loads. Although bottom-up methods improve forecasting accuracy by integrating appliance-level data, monitoring all appliances is costly, and many do not meaningfully impact total load prediction. Therefore, we propose GCA-BULF, a bottom-up short-term load forecasting framework based on grouped critical appliances, supported by three key designs. First, the Critical Appliance Filtering module ranks appliances according to their power consumption, switching frequency, and usage pattern periodicity, and identifies critical ones through iterative load decomposition. Next, the Related Appliance Grouping module clusters these appliances based on spatial and temporal correlations for group-level forecasting. Finally, the Collaborative Load Forecasting module refines the total load prediction by combining multiple group-level forecasts. We evaluate GCA-BULF on residential and office building load forecasting tasks. Experimental results reveal that GCA-BULF improves hourly total load forecasting by 20.85%-57.88% compared to existing top-down methods and by 33.03%-92.48% compared to bottom-up methods.

</details>


### [155] [Automated detection of pediatric congenital heart disease from phonocardiograms using deep and handcrafted feature fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24767)
*Abdul Jabbar,Ethan Grooby,Yang Yi Poh,Khawza I. Ahmad,Md Hassanuzzaman,Raqibul Mostafa,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Faezeh Marzbanrad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于深度特征融合的方法，利用数字听诊器（PCG录音）进行先天性心脏病（CHD）的自动化早期检测，在751名儿科患者中达到92%准确率和96% AUROC，可作为低资源地区的成本效益筛查工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先天性心脏病影响全球约1%活产儿， echocardiography作为金标准诊断方法成本高、在低资源地区难以普及，且由于专家解读能力差异导致诊断延迟和变异性大，需要更易获取的诊断方式。

Method: 收集751名儿科受试者（1月-16岁）来自孟加拉国的PCG录音，在四个听诊部位（MV、AV、PV、TV）采集，由心脏病专家确认标注为CHD或非CHD。采用深度特征融合方法，整合深度特征与手工特征，采用患者-wise划分（70%训练、20%验证、10%测试）。

Result: 模型达到92%准确率、91%灵敏度、91%特异性、96% AUROC、92% F1-score。

Conclusion: 该模型可实现高效的实时远程CHD检测，作为低资源地区的成本效益筛查工具具有良好前景。

Abstract: Congenital heart disease (CHD) is the most common type of birth defect, impacting about 1% of live births worldwide. Echocardiography, the gold-standard diagnostic method, is costly and inaccessible in low-resource settings. Diagnosis is delayed due to limited skilled experts, whose ability to interpret pathological patterns varies significantly, causing inter- and intra-clinician variability. Therefore, we present a new method for a more accessible diagnostic modality, the digital stethoscope, to detect CHDs. Our method is based on deep feature fusion, integrating deep and handcrafted features for the automated early detection of CHDs. For this work, Phonocardiography (PCG) recordings were obtained from 751 pediatric subjects (Age:1 month- 16 years) in Bangladesh, ranging from infants to adults at four auscultation locations: mitral valve (MV), aortic valve (AV), pulmonary valve (PV), and tricuspid valve (TV). These recordings were labeled based on confirmed diagnoses by cardiologists as either cases of CHD or non-CHD. The results demonstrated that our proposed model achieved an accuracy of 92%, a sensitivity of 91%, and a specificity of 91%, based on a patient-wise split of 70% training, 20% validation, and 10% testing. Furthermore, the Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 96%, and an F1-score of 92%. This model promises efficient real-time remote detection of CHDs as a cost-effective screening tool for low-resource settings.

</details>


### [156] [Comparative Study of Bending Analysis using Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Numerical Dynamic Deflection in Perforated nanobeam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24768)
*Ramanath Garai,Iswari Sahu,S. Chakraverty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This chapter presents a DFL-TFC (Domain-mapped Functional Link Constrained Framework) method for analyzing bending behavior of perforated nanobeams under sinusoidal loading. The method combines functional link neural networks with domain mapping to orthogonal polynomials, achieving accurate static-dynamic deflection relationship analysis while ensuring strict boundary condition satisfaction without deep network architectures.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The research aims to determine the relationship between static bending response and dynamic deflection of perforated nanobeams for various perforation cases under sinusoidal loading, while addressing the complexity of deep neural network architectures required by standard Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINN).

Method: The approach uses Theory of Functional Connections (TFC) to embed differential equation constraints into a Constrained Expression (CE) that exactly satisfies initial and boundary conditions. The free function is expressed through a Functional Link Neural Network (FLNN), with cost minimized by mean square residual of the differential equation. Static bending is obtained via FL-TFC with Domain Mapping, while dynamic deflection is determined using the Galerkin method.

Result: The method successfully investigated the relationship between static and dynamic deflection of simply-supported (S-S) perforated nanobeams, demonstrating that the FL-TFC approach maintains accuracy and efficiency without requiring complex neural network architectures.

Conclusion: FL-TFC with Domain Mapping method provides an efficient, computationally robust, and accurate alternative to standard PINN. It successfully eliminates the need for deep and complex neural network architectures while ensuring strict satisfaction of boundary conditions.

Abstract: In this chapter, we investigate the bending behavior of a perforated nanobeam subjected to sinusoidal loading using an efficient and computationally robust Physics-Informed Functional Link Constrained Framework with Domain Mapping (DFL-TFC) method. Our aim is to determine the relationship between static bending response and dynamic deflection of a perforated nanobeam for various perforation cases. The static bending is obtained using the FL-TFC with Domain mapped method, whereas dynamic deflection is determined using the Galerkin method. The proposed approach employs the theory of functional connections (TFC) to systematically embed governing differential equation constraints into a constrained expression (CE), which exactly satisfies all prescribed initial and boundary conditions (ICs and BCs) and domain of differential equation is mapped to domain of orthogonal polynomials. Within this framework, the free function appearing in the constrained expression is expressed through a functional link neural network (FLNN). The cost is minimized by the mean square residual of DE, allowing training without requiring complex deep network architectures. Relationship between static and dynamic defection of simply-supported (S-S) perforated nanobeams has been investigated here. FL-TFC with Domain mapped method eliminates the need for deep and complex neural network architectures while ensuring accuracy, efficiency, and strict satisfaction of boundary conditions as compared to standard PINN.

</details>


### [157] [Prior-Aligned Data Cleaning for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25154)
*Laure Berti-Equille*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: L2C2是首个将表格数据清洗转化为先验对齐问题的深度强化学习框架，通过学习性策略序列化清洗操作符来弥合脏数据与TFM合成先验之间的分布差距。实验表明，精心设计的TFMAwareReward在部分数据集上实现更高精度，参数化动作显著提升管道质量，预训练策略展现出良好的跨数据集迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tabular Foundation Models（TFMs）在小规模表格数据集上通过元学习实现SOTA零样本精度，但其上下文学习机制假设输入数据相对干净。真实数据中的缺失值、异常值和重复数据会导致先验不匹配，同时降低准确率和置信度校准能力。

Method: 提出L2C2——首个深度强化学习框架，将表格数据清洗建模为先验对齐问题。学习策略（policy）通过序列化操作符（operators）来最小化脏数据与TFM合成先验之间的分布差距。

Result: 1. 7种奖励设计中3种退化为平凡清洗策略——奖励工程具有科学挑战性；2. TFMAwareReward在4/10数据集上选择结构不同管道，这些情况下TabPFN精度更高（均值0.851 vs 0.843，Wilcoxon p=0.063）；3. 参数化清洗动作在9/10数据集上改善管道奖励（Wilcoxon p=0.004）；4. 在单一源数据集上预训练的策略，在所有三个保留数据集上超越从零训练（微调2000步后最高+28.8%）。

Conclusion: Prior alignment（先验对齐）是一种用于TFM在真实表格数据部署中的原则性数据准备策略。L2C2通过深度强化学习框架，成功将表格数据清洗建模为先验对齐问题，证明了预训练清洗策略的跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) achieve state-of-the-art zero-shot accuracy on small tabular datasets by meta-learning over synthetic data-generating processes -- making them highly attractive for practitioners who cannot afford large annotated corpora. However, their in-context learning mechanism assumes approximately clean inputs: missing values, outliers, and duplicates in the real-world data create a prior mismatch that degrades both accuracy and confidence calibration simultaneously. Correcting this mismatch requires sequential decisions over cleaning operators whose interactions no static preprocessing rule can anticipate -a natural fit for reinforcement learning~(RL). We introduce L2C2, the first deep RL framework framing tabular data cleaning as prior alignment: a learned policy sequences operators to minimize the distributional gap between dirty input and the TFM's synthetic prior. Six experiments on ten OpenML benchmark datasets establish: 1) three of seven reward designs collapse to degenerate trivial cleaning strategies -- principled reward engineering is scientifically non-trivial; 2) the novel TFMAwareReward reward we propose selects structurally distinct pipelines on 4/10 datasets and achieves higher TabPFN accuracy on those diverging cases (mean 0.851 vs. 0.843; Wilcoxon p=0.063, n=4) while never underperforming; 3) parameterized cleaning actions improve best-found pipeline reward on 9/10 datasets (Wilcoxon p=0.004); and 4) a policy pre-trained on one single source dataset exceeds scratch training at the 2,000-step fine-tuning checkpoint on all three held-out datasets (up to +28.8% after full fine-tuning) demonstrating cross-dataset transfer of prior-alignment knowledge. These findings establish that prior alignment is a principled data preparation strategy for TFM deployment on real-world tabular data.

</details>


### [158] [Architecture Determines Observability in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24801)
*Thomas Carmichael*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了自回归Transformer中"可观测性"(Observability)的概念——即从中间层激活中线性读取每个token决策质量的能力。研究发现，某些模型配置（如Pythia的24层16头）在训练中会"崩溃"，丢失这种可观测性，而其他配置则保持健康水平。置信度和激活范数等控制变量的校正至关重要，可吸收57.7%的原始探测信号。跨架构实验表明，Qwen 2.5和Llama在相同3B规模下可观测性差异达2.9倍，且可观测性可跨任务迁移，无需在目标任务上训练即可捕获10.9-13.4%的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前依赖max-softmax置信度的错误检测方法存在局限性，因为模型可能产生高置信度的错误预测。研究者希望从transformer的中间层激活中提取内部信号来检测此类"自信错误"，但这需要模型内部保留决策质量的可读信号。该研究旨在系统性地定义和度量这种"可观测性"属性。

Method: 研究使用线性探针(liear probes)从冻结的中间层激活中预测每个token的决策质量，并在控制max-softmax置信度和激活范数后评估信号强度(ρ_partial)。实验覆盖13个模型、6个模型家族，包括Pythia的受控训练套件以及Qwen、Llama、Mistral等独立训练的模型。WikiText训练的观测器被迁移到下游QA任务进行评估。

Result: 可观测性非transformer的通用属性，而是由架构和训练配方共同决定。Pythia的24层16头配置在训练中完全崩溃(ρ_partial ~ 0.10)，而其他六种配置保持在0.21-0.38的健康范围。跨模型实验中，Qwen 2.5和Llama在3B规模下差异2.9倍，Mistral 7B保持可观测性而Llama 3.1 8B崩溃。WikiText训练的观测器在20%标记率下可额外捕获10.9-13.4%的错误（九分之七的模型-任务组合）。

Conclusion: 可观测性是transformer架构和训练配方共同决定的离散属性，而非渐进可调的连续参数。崩溃在训练中涌现，且不同架构在同一参数量下可能表现截然不同。架构选择本身就是一种错误监控决策——选择正确架构可实现无需额外训练的跨任务错误检测能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformers make confident errors, but activation monitoring can catch them only if the model preserves an internal signal that output confidence does not expose. This preservation is determined by architecture and training recipe. We define observability as the linear readability of per-token decision quality from frozen mid-layer activations after controlling for max-softmax confidence and activation norm. The correction is essential. Confidence controls absorb 57.7% of raw probe signal on average across 13 models in 6 families.
  Observability is not a generic property of transformers. In Pythia's controlled suite, every tested run with the 24-layer, 16-head configuration collapses to rho_partial ~0.10 across a 3.5x parameter gap and two Pile variants, while six other configurations occupy a separated healthy band from 0.21 to 0.38. The output-controlled residual collapses at the same points, and neither tested nonlinear probes nor layer sweeps recover healthy-range signal. Checkpoint dynamics show the collapse is emergent during training. Both configurations at matched hidden dimension form the signal at the earliest measured checkpoint, but training erases it in the (24L, 16H) class while predictive loss continues improving.
  Across independent recipes the collapse map changes but the phenomenon persists. Qwen 2.5 and Llama differ by 2.9x at matched 3B scale with probe seed distributions that do not overlap, while Mistral 7B preserves observability where Llama 3.1 8B collapses despite similar broad architecture. A WikiText-trained observer transfers to downstream QA without training on those tasks, catching errors confidence misses. At 20% flag rate, its exclusive catch rate is 10.9-13.4% of all errors in seven of nine model-task cells. Architecture selection is a monitoring decision.

</details>


### [159] [Intrinsic Mutual Information as a Modulator for Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24804)
*Peng Liao,Peijia Zheng,Lingbo Li,Shangsong Liang,Lin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RMiPO是一种用于大语言模型对齐的轻量级离线偏好优化框架，通过利用响应级互信息动态解耦偏好贡献，在无需额外超参数调优的情况下，实现更优的性能并降低超过15%的训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线偏好优化方法（如DPO）在实现最佳性能时需要额外的超参数调优，导致大量时间开销，且现有改进方法效果有限，仍未完全消除对超参数调优的依赖。

Method: RMiPO框架利用内在的响应级互信息（Response-level Mutual Information）进行偏好优化，通过超参数调制，在可忽略的计算成本下动态解耦偏好贡献。

Result: 大量实验结果表明，RMiPO始终优于现有方法，且训练开销降低超过15%。

Conclusion: RMiPO提供了一个轻量级、高效的离线偏好优化解决方案，通过动态调节偏好贡献的机制，成功减少了对超参数调优的依赖，同时保持了性能优势。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization methods, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), offer significant advantages in aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human values. However, achieving optimal performance with these methods typically involves additional hyperparameter tuning, resulting in substantial time overhead. Although prior work has proposed a range of improvements, these methods remain limited in effectiveness and have not fully eliminated reliance on hyperparameter tuning. In this work, we propose RMiPO, a lightweight and efficient framework for offline preference optimization. RMiPO leverages intrinsic Response-level Mutual information for Preference Optimization with hyperparameter modulation, dynamically decoupling preference contributions at negligible additional computational cost. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that RMiPO achieves consistently superior performance over existing methods while reducing training overhead by more than 15\%. Our code is available at https://github.com/liavonpenn/rmipo.

</details>


### [160] [PolyKV: A Shared Asymmetrically-Compressed KV Cache Pool for Multi-Agent LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24971)
*Ishan Patel,Ishan Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present PolyKV, a system in which multiple concurrent inference agents share a single, asymmetrically compressed KV cache pool. Rather than allocating a separate KV cache per agent -- the standard paradigm -- PolyKV writes a compressed cache once and injects it into N independent agent contexts via HuggingFace DynamicCache objects.
